212: @pytest.mark.ams
213: def test_qracc_ams(
214:     col_symmetric,
215:     simulator,
216:     seed,
217:     weight_mode,
218:     figures,
219:     wDimX = 32, #nColumns
220:     wDimY = 128, #nRows
221:     xBatches = 10,
222:     xTrits = 1,
223:     outBits = 4,
224:     snr_limit = 8,
225:     run = True, # Set to False to skip RTL simulation
226:     x_repeat = False,
227: ):
228:     """AMS co-simulation test for the qr_acc_wrapper block."""
229: 
230:     w,x,wx_outBits = generate_qracc_inputs(
231:         wDimX = wDimX,
232:         wDimY = wDimY,
233:         xBatches = xBatches,
234:         xTrits = xTrits,
235:         outBits = outBits,
236:         seed = seed,
237:         weight_mode = weight_mode,
238:         col_symmetric = col_symmetric,
239:         rangeBits = 5,
240:         x_repeat = x_repeat,

242:     )

109: def pytest_collection_modifyitems(config, items):
110:     heavy = config.getoption("--heavy")
111:     proprietary = config.getoption("--proprietary")
112:     verilator_available = shutil.which('verilator') is not None
113:     for item in items:
114:         # proprietary marker
115:         if item.get_closest_marker("proprietary") and not proprietary:
116:             item.add_marker(pytest.mark.skip(reason="proprietary test: needs Cadence/Synopsys tools or synthesized netlists; run with --proprietary"))
117:         # ams marker: needs analog co-simulation outputs that are not in the repo
118:         if item.get_closest_marker("ams") and not proprietary:
119:             item.add_marker(pytest.mark.skip(reason="ams test: needs AMS co-simulation outputs in tb/*/inputs (not in repo); run with --proprietary"))
120:         # heavy marker
121:         if item.get_closest_marker("heavy") and not heavy:
122:             item.add_marker(pytest.mark.skip(reason="heavy test: long full-model simulations; run with --heavy"))
123:         # needs_verilator marker
124:         if item.get_closest_marker("needs_verilator") and not verilator_available:
125:             item.add_marker(pytest.mark.skip(reason="verilator not found on PATH (install verilator or run inside the devcontainer)"))
126:         # simulator parameter: skip only for tests parametrized by `simulator`

135: def _normalize_cifar(path):
136:     img = np.asarray(Image.open(path).convert('RGB'), dtype=np.float32) / 255.0
137:     mean = np.array((0.4914, 0.4822, 0.4465), dtype=np.float32)
138:     std = np.array((0.2023, 0.1994, 0.2010), dtype=np.float32)
139:     img = (img - mean) / std
140:     return img.transpose(2, 0, 1)[np.newaxis]
141:

104: def test_pack_weight_planes_reconstruct_quantized():
105:     """Emitter words (B=2,4,8) unpack and recombine to quantize_int8_to_bits output."""
106:     from qracc_marp.compile.compile import pack_weight_planes
107:

109:     w = rng.integers(-128, 128, size=(32, 32), dtype=np.int8)
110:     n_bits = w.size
111:     for B in (2, 4, 8):
112:         words = pack_weight_planes(w, B=B, signed=True)
113:         # Words are flat [bank_row, plane]: index i -> bank row i//8, plane i%8.
114:         planes_out = np.zeros((B, n_bits), dtype=np.uint8)
115:         for i, word in enumerate(words):
116:             plane = i % 8
117:             bank_row = i // 8
118:             if plane >= B:
119:                 continue
120:             for c in range(32):
121:                 f = bank_row * 32 + c
122:                 if f < n_bits:
123:                     planes_out[plane, f] = (int(word) >> c) & 1
124:         planes_out = planes_out.reshape((B,) + w.shape)
125:         r = np.zeros_like(w, dtype=np.int64)
126:         for b in range(B):
127:             term = planes_out[b].astype(np.int64) * (1 << b)
128:             if b == B - 1:  # MSB / sign bit is negated for signed
129:                 r = r - term
130:             else:
131:                 r = r + term
132:         q = quant.quantize_int8_to_bits(w, B, signed=True)
133:         assert np.array_equal(r, q)

153:     planes_out = np.zeros((8, n_bits), dtype=np.uint8)
154:     for i, word in enumerate(words):
155:         plane = i % 8
156:         bank_row = i // 8
157:         for c in range(32):
158:             f = bank_row * 32 + c
159:             if f < n_bits:
160:                 planes_out[plane, f] = (int(word) >> c) & 1
161:     planes_out = planes_out.reshape((8,) + w.shape)
162:     r = np.zeros_like(w, dtype=np.int64)
163:     for b in range(8):
164:         term = planes_out[b].astype(np.int64) * (1 << b)
165:         if b == 7:  # MSB / sign bit is negated for signed
166:             r = r - term
167:         else:
168:             r = r + term
169:     assert np.array_equal(r, w.astype(np.int64))

392: def make_packers():
393:     """Return a fresh dict of rectpack packers (one instance per name).
394: 
395:     Packer objects are stateful, so a new instance is created on every call
396:     to avoid cross-model contamination.
397:     """
398:     import rectpack
399:     from qracc_marp.mapping import packer_utils as pu
400:     return {
401:         "Dense": rectpack.newPacker(
402:             mode=rectpack.PackingMode.Offline,
403:             rotation=False,
404:             pack_algo=rectpack.MaxRectsBssf
405:         ),
406:         "Naive": pu.NaiveRectpackPacker(256, 256, rotation=False),
407:         "Tradeoff": rectpack.newPacker(
408:             mode=rectpack.PackingMode.Online,
409:             bin_algo=rectpack.PackingBin.BBF,
410:             rotation=False,
411:             pack_algo=rectpack.MaxRectsBssf
412:         ),
413:         "Write Optimized": rectpack.newPacker(
414:             mode=rectpack.PackingMode.Online,
415:             bin_algo=rectpack.PackingBin.BNF,
416:             rotation=False,
417:             pack_algo=rectpack.MaxRectsBssf
418:         ),
419:     }
420:

44: def pack_weight_planes(weights, B, signed):
45:     """Pack per-plane weight bit-words for the B>1 emitter.
46: 
47:     ``weight_bit_planes`` yields 8 planes (b < B real, b >= B zero). Each
48:     plane is flattened, padded to rows of 32 columns, and each row has its
49:     columns reversed and is packed MSB-first, so matrix column j ends at word
50:     bit j (same convention as stimulus_gen.mapped_matrix_to_bank_writes).
51:     Words are
52:     returned flat in ``[bank_row, plane]`` order so word index ``i`` maps to
53:     bank row ``i // 8`` and plane ``i % 8``; planes b >= weight_bits are zero
54:     words ignored by the shift-add tree (b >= weight_bits_i contributes 0).
55:     """
56:     planes = quant.weight_bit_planes(weights, B, signed)
57:     n_plane = planes.shape[0]
58:     flat = planes.reshape(n_plane, -1)
59:     pad = (-flat.shape[1]) % 32
60:     if pad:
61:         flat = np.pad(flat, ((0, 0), (0, pad)), constant_values=0)
62:     rows = flat.reshape(n_plane, -1, 32)
63:     words = quant.array_bin_to_int(rows.reshape(-1, 32)[:, ::-1])
64:     words = words.reshape(n_plane, -1).transpose().reshape(-1)
65:     return words
66:

204:     @classmethod

206:         cls,

220:         nx_node, nx_model, ifmap = sample_onnx_qlinearconv(ifmap_shape=ifmap_shape,ifmap_bits=ifmap_bits,kernel_shape=kernel_shape,kernel_bits=kernel_bits,kernel_dtype = np.int8,pads = pads,stride = stride, seed = 0, depthwise = depthwise)
221: 
222:         mapped_node = core.MappedNode(nx_node, bin_id = 0, mapped_rect=None, nx_model = nx_model, offset_x=offset_x, offset_y = offset_y)
223: 
224:         bin_weights = np.zeros(core_size, dtype=np.int8)
225:         bin_weights[offset_y:offset_y+mapped_node.matrix.shape[0], offset_x:offset_x+mapped_node.matrix.shape[1]] = mapped_node.matrix

239:         )
240:

243:     def _get_weight_data(self):
244:         if self.mapped_node.depthwise:
245: 
246:             if self.mapped_node.kernel.shape[0] < self.ws_core_size:
247:                 physical_kernel = np.zeros((self.ws_core_size, *self.mapped_node.kernel.shape[1:]), dtype=self.mapped_node.kernel.dtype)
248:                 physical_kernel[:self.mapped_node.kernel.shape[0]] = self.mapped_node.kernel
249:             else:
250:                 physical_kernel = self.mapped_node.kernel
251: 
252:             physical_kernel_hwc = physical_kernel.transpose(1,2,3,0)  # Convert to KHWC format
253:             return kernel_to_writes(physical_kernel_hwc, channels=self.ws_core_size, hexes=False)
254:         else:
255:             if self.weight_bits > 1:
256:                 return pack_weight_planes(self.mapped_bin.weights, B=self.weight_bits, signed=self.weight_signed)
257:             return mapped_matrix_to_bank_writes(self.mapped_bin.weights, 32)
258:         
259:     def _get_scaler_data(self):
260:         return pad_scaler_writes(
261:             scale_to_map = self.mapped_node.scale,
262:             core_shape= self.imc_core_size,
263:             output_zero_point = self.mapped_node.y_zp,
264:             mm_offset_x= self.mapped_node.offset_x,
265:         )
266:     
267:     def _get_bias_data(self):
268:         return pad_bias_data(self.mapped_node.biases, 
269:             core_shape=self.imc_core_size, 
270:             mm_offset_x=self.mapped_node.offset_x,
271:         )
272:     
273:     def _get_ifmap_data(self):
274:         ifmap_hwc = self.ifmap.transpose(0,2,3,1)
275:         return pack_ifmap_to_ints(ifmap_hwc)
276: 
277:     def write_files(self, savepath):
278:         '''
279:         Write reference text files for the QRAcc RTL testbench.
280:         Optional. Only used to debug.
281:         '''
282:         
283:         res_dict = {
284:             'result': self.reference_output,
285:             'toeplitz': self.toeplitz,
286:             'ifmap': self.ifmap.transpose(0,2,3,1),  # Convert to NHWC format
287:             'matrix_raw': self.matrix,  
288:             'scaler_data': self._get_scaler_data(),
289:             'biases': self._get_bias_data()
290:         }
291: 
292:         write_input_files(res_dict, savepath)

294:     def _generate_reference_output(self):
295:         node_initializers = [
296:             self.ifmap,
297:             self.mapped_node.x_scale,
298:             self.mapped_node.x_zp,
299:             self.mapped_node.kernel,
300:             self.mapped_node.w_scale,
301:             self.mapped_node.w_zp,
302:             self.mapped_node.y_scale,
303:             self.mapped_node.y_zp,
304:             self.mapped_node.biases
305:         ]
306:         sample_outs = np.random.randint(
307:             low=0, high=2**self.ofmap_bits, size=self.ofmap_shape, dtype=np.uint8
308:         )
309:         return onnx_utils.infer_node_output(
310:             self.mapped_node.nx_node,
311:             inputs=node_initializers,
312:             outputs=[sample_outs],
313:             name=self.mapped_node.name,
314:         )        
315:     
316:     def compile(
317:         self,
318:         include_ifmap_writes: bool = True,
319:         write_weights: bool = True,
320:         add_read: bool = True,
321:         config_write_address: str = CSR_BASE_ADDR_HEX,
322:         end: bool = True,
323:         preserve_ifmap: bool = False,
324:     ) -> list[str]:
325:         '''
326:         Compile the node into a list of assembly instructions for QRAcc.
327:         include_ifmap_writes: bool, whether to include ifmap writes in the output.
328:         solo: bool, whether to compile the node as a standalone unit (default True).
329:         '''
330:         # print(f"Compiling node {self.mapped_node.node_id}:{self.mapped_node.name} for QRAcc...")
331: 
332:         config_dict = self.config()
333:         config_writes = bundle_config_into_write(config_dict, config_write_address)
334:         commands = config_writes
335:         
336:         if write_weights:
337:             if self.mapped_node.depthwise:
338:                 commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_LOADWEIGHTS_DIGITAL', write_address=config_write_address)
339:             else:
340:                 commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_LOADWEIGHTS', write_address=config_write_address)
341:             if self.weight_bits > 1:
342:                 commands += write_weight_rows_to_asm(self._get_weight_data(), signed=False)
343:             else:
344:                 commands += write_array_to_asm(self._get_weight_data()) 
345:         
346:         # Writing to the scaler is not optional
347:         commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_LOAD_SCALER', write_address=config_write_address)
348:         commands += write_array_to_asm(self._get_scaler_data()) 
349:         commands += write_array_to_asm(self._get_bias_data()) 
350:         
351:         if include_ifmap_writes: # If not, the ifmap is assumed to be already in the ACTMEM
352:             commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_LOAD_ACTIVATION', write_address=config_write_address)
353:         commands += write_array_to_asm(self._get_ifmap_data())
354: 
355:         if self.mapped_node.depthwise:
356:             commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_COMPUTE_DIGITAL', write_address=config_write_address, preserve_ifmap=preserve_ifmap)
357:         else:
358:             commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_COMPUTE_ANALOG', write_address=config_write_address, preserve_ifmap=preserve_ifmap)
359:         commands += [f'WAITBUSY']
360:         
361:         if add_read:
362:             commands += make_trigger_write('TRIGGER_READ_ACTIVATION', write_address=config_write_address)
363:             commands += [f'WAITREAD']
364:             if end:
365:                 commands += ['END']
366: 
367:         return commands
368:     
369:     def __repr__(self):
370:         # Print attributes of the class, showing shapes for arrays
371:         attrs = vars(self)
372:         attr_strs = []
373:         for key, value in attrs.items():
374:             if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
375:                 attr_strs.append(f"{key}=array(shape={value.shape})")
376:             elif key == 'mapped_node':
377:                 attr_strs.append(f"{key}={value.name} (id={value.node_id})")
378:             elif key == 'mapped_bin' and value is not None:
379:                 attr_strs.append(f"{key}={value.bin_id} (shape={value.weights.shape})")
380:             elif key == 'nx_model':
381:                 attr_strs.append(f"{key}={value.graph.name} (nodes={len(value.graph.node)})")
382:             else:
383:                 attr_strs.append(f"{key}={value}")
384:         attr_list = ',\n\t'.join(attr_strs)
385:         return f"QrAccNodeCode(\n\t{attr_list})"
386: 
387: def generate_random_intermediate_tensor(
388:     nx_model : onnx.ModelProto,
389:     node_id : int,
390:     input_dict : dict
391: ):
392:     input_tensor_shape = onnx_utils.get_intermediate_tensor_value(
393:         nx_model, nx_model.graph.node[node_id].input[0], input_dict=input_dict).shape
394:     return np.random.randint(0, 256, input_tensor_shape).astype(np.uint8)
395: 
396: def is_nx_node_compilable(
397:     nx_node : onnx.NodeProto
398: ):
399:     """
400:     Check if an ONNX node is compilable for QRAcc.
401:     Currently, only QLinearConv and QLinearMatMul nodes are supported.
402:     """
403:     if nx_node is None:
404:         return False
405:     if nx_node.op_type == 'QLinearConv':
406:         return True
407:     elif nx_node.op_type == 'QLinearMatMul':
408:         return True
409:     else:
410:         return False
411: 
412: def make_trigger_write(
413:     command: str,
414:     clear: int = 0,
415:     inst_write_mode: int = 0,
416:     csr_main_trigger_enum: dict[str, int] | None = None,
417:     write_address: str = CSR_BASE_ADDR_HEX,
418:     preserve_ifmap: bool = False,
419: ) -> list[str]:
420:     """Build a single LOAD instruction that writes a trigger word to the CSR.
421: 
422:     Args:
423:         command: Trigger name (e.g. ``'TRIGGER_COMPUTE_ANALOG'``).
424:         clear: Clear bit (0 or 1).
425:         inst_write_mode: Instruction write-mode bit.
426:         csr_main_trigger_enum: Optional custom trigger→value mapping.
427:         write_address: Hex-string target address.
428:         preserve_ifmap: If *True* the ifmap is kept in ACTMEM.
429:     """
430:     if csr_main_trigger_enum is None:
431:         csr_main_trigger_enum = TRIGGER_ENUM_MAP
432:     if command not in csr_main_trigger_enum:
433:         raise ValueError(f"Unknown command: {command}")
434: 
435:     trigger_val = csr_main_trigger_enum[command] & 0x7
436:     clear_val = (clear & MASK_1) << 3
437:     inst_write_mode_val = (inst_write_mode & MASK_1) << 5
438:     preserve_ifmap_val = (int(preserve_ifmap) & MASK_1) << 12
439: 
440:     word = trigger_val | clear_val | inst_write_mode_val | preserve_ifmap_val
441:     return [f'LOAD {write_address} {word:08x}']
442: 
443: def bundle_config_into_write(
444:     config_dict: dict[str, int],
445:     config_write_address: str = CSR_BASE_ADDR_HEX,
446: ) -> list[str]:
447:     """Pack QRAcc configuration fields into seven 32-bit CSR LOAD instructions."""
448:     base_addr = int(config_write_address, 16)
449: 
450:     config_word = (
451:         ((config_dict['n_output_bits_cfg']      & MASK_4) << 28) |
452:         ((config_dict['n_input_bits_cfg']       & MASK_4) << 24) |
453:         ((config_dict['stride_y']               & MASK_4) << 20) |
454:         ((config_dict['stride_x']               & MASK_4) << 16) |
455:         ((config_dict['filter_size_x']          & MASK_4) << 12) |
456:         ((config_dict['filter_size_y']          & MASK_4) << 8)  |
457:         ((config_dict['adc_ref_range_shifts']   & MASK_4) << 4)  |
458:         ((config_dict['unsigned_acts']          & MASK_1) << 1)  |
459:         ((config_dict['binary_cfg']             & MASK_1) << 0)
460:     )
461:     ifmap_dims_word = (
462:         ((config_dict['input_fmap_dimy'] & MASK_16) << 16) |
463:         ((config_dict['input_fmap_dimx'] & MASK_16) << 0)
464:     )
465:     ofmap_dims_word = (
466:         ((config_dict['output_fmap_dimy'] & MASK_16) << 16) |
467:         ((config_dict['output_fmap_dimx'] & MASK_16) << 0)
468:     )
469:     channels_word = (
470:         ((config_dict['num_output_channels'] & MASK_16) << 16) |
471:         ((config_dict['num_input_channels']  & MASK_16) << 0)
472:     )
473:     offsets_word = (
474:         ((config_dict['mapped_matrix_offset_y'] & MASK_16) << 16) |
475:         ((config_dict['mapped_matrix_offset_x'] & MASK_16) << 0)
476:     )
477:     padding_word = (
478:         ((int(config_dict['padding_value']) & MASK_16) << 4) |
479:         ((config_dict['padding'] & MASK_16) << 0)
480:     )
481:     weight_word = (
482:         ((config_dict.get('weight_bits', 1) & 0xF) << 0) |
483:         ((config_dict.get('weight_signed', 0) & 0x1) << 4)
484:     )
485: 
486:     config_writes = [
487:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_CONFIG:08x} {config_word:08x}",
488:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_IFMAP_DIMS:08x} {ifmap_dims_word:08x}",
489:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_OFMAP_DIMS:08x} {ofmap_dims_word:08x}",
490:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_CHANNELS:08x} {channels_word:08x}",
491:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_OFFSETS:08x} {offsets_word:08x}",
492:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_PADDING:08x} {padding_word:08x}",
493:         f"LOAD {base_addr + CSR_REG_WEIGHT:08x} {weight_word:08x}",
494:     ]
495: 
496:     return config_writes
497: 
498: def write_array_to_asm(write_array: np.ndarray | list, address: str = DATA_WRITE_ADDR) -> list[str]:
499:     """Convert a data array into a list of LOAD assembly instructions."""
500:     asm = []
501:     for element in write_array:
502:         asm.append(f"LOAD {address} {vhex3(element)}")
503:     return asm
504: 
505: def get_config_from_mapped_node(
506:     mapped_node : core.MappedNode,
507:     ifmap_shape : tuple,
508:     ifmap_bits = 8,
509:     ofmap_bits = 8
510: ):
511:     '''
512:     Generate QRAcc configuration for a QLinearConv node.
513:     Needs information about the previous ifmap shape.
514:     '''
515: 
516:     if ifmap_shape[1] != mapped_node.kernel.shape[1]:
517:         raise ValueError("Input feature map channels do not match kernel input channels.")
518: 
519:     kernel_shape = mapped_node.kernel.shape
520:     ofmap_dimx = ((ifmap_shape[2] - kernel_shape[2] + 2*mapped_node.pads[0]) // mapped_node.strides[0]) + 1 #(W-K+2P)/S + 1
521:     ofmap_dimy = ((ifmap_shape[3] - kernel_shape[3] + 2*mapped_node.pads[1]) // mapped_node.strides[1]) + 1
522: 
523:     tplitz_window_length = kernel_shape[2] * kernel_shape[3] * ifmap_shape[1] # CFxFy
524: 
525:     tplitz_act_vector = np.random.randint(
526:         low=0, high=2**ifmap_bits, size=tplitz_window_length)
527: 
528:     adc_ref_range_shifts = infer_optimal_adc_range_shifts(
529:         tplitz_act_vector=tplitz_act_vector,
530:         weights=mapped_node.matrix.T,
531:         ifmap_bits=ifmap_bits
532:     )
533: 
534:     config_dict = {
535:         "n_input_bits_cfg": ifmap_bits,
536:         "n_output_bits_cfg": ofmap_bits,
537:         "unsigned_acts": 1,
538:         "binary_cfg": 1,
539:         "adc_ref_range_shifts": int(adc_ref_range_shifts),
540:         "filter_size_y": kernel_shape[3],
541:         "filter_size_x": kernel_shape[2],
542:         "input_fmap_dimx": ifmap_shape[2],
543:         "input_fmap_dimy": ifmap_shape[3],
544:         "output_fmap_dimx": ofmap_dimx,
545:         "output_fmap_dimy": ofmap_dimy,
546:         "stride_x": mapped_node.strides[0],
547:         "stride_y": mapped_node.strides[1],
548:         "num_input_channels": ifmap_shape[1],
549:         "num_output_channels": kernel_shape[0],
550:         "mapped_matrix_offset_x": mapped_node.offset_x,
551:         "mapped_matrix_offset_y": mapped_node.offset_y,
552:         "padding": mapped_node.pads[0],  # Use 0 padding for soft padding
553:         "padding_value": mapped_node.x_zp,  # Padding value for the input feature map
554:     }
555: 
556:     return config_dict
557: 
558: def check_if_any_consumers_are_noncompilable(
559:     nx_model : onnx.ModelProto,
560:     nx_node : int
561: ):
562:     """
563:     Check if any consumers of the given node are non-compilable.
564:     Returns True if any consumer is non-compilable, False otherwise.
565:     """
566:     for output in nx_node.output:
567:         for node in nx_model.graph.node:
568:             if output in node.input and not is_nx_node_compilable(node):
569:                 return True
570:     return False
571: 
572: def traverse_and_compile_nx_graph(
573:     nx_model     : onnx.ModelProto,
574:     input_dict   : dict,
575:     imc_core_size: tuple = (256, 256),
576:     dwc_core_size: int = 32,
577:     until        : int = None,
578:     starting     : int = 0,
579:     packer = None
580: ):
581:     
582:     u_nx_mapping = core.NxModelMapping(
583:         nx_model,
584:         imc_core_size=imc_core_size,
585:         dwc_core_size=dwc_core_size,
586:         packer = packer
587:     )
588: 
589:     if until is None:
590:         until = len(nx_model.graph.node)
591:     commands = []
592: 
593:     prev_bin_id = None
594:     current_loaded_ifmap_name = None
595:     for node_id,nx_node in enumerate(nx_model.graph.node[starting:until]):
596:         
597:         readout = check_if_any_consumers_are_noncompilable(nx_model, nx_node)
598:         next_node = nx_model.graph.node[node_id + 1] if node_id + 1 < len(nx_model.graph.node) else None
599: 
600:         if is_nx_node_compilable(nx_node):
601:             input_tensor = generate_random_intermediate_tensor(
602:                 nx_model, node_id, input_dict
603:             )
604:             u_code = QrAccNodeCode(
605:                 mapped_node   = u_nx_mapping.get_mapped_node_by_id(node_id),
606:                 mapped_bin    = u_nx_mapping.get_bin_of_node_id(node_id),
607:                 ifmap         = input_tensor,
608:                 imc_core_size = imc_core_size,
609:                 ws_core_size  = dwc_core_size,
610:                 nx_model      = nx_model
611:             )
612: 
613:             preserve_ifmap = False
614:             if is_nx_node_compilable(next_node) and next_node.input[0] == u_code.inputs[0]:
615:                 preserve_ifmap = True
616: 
617:             commands += ['INFO']
618:             commands += [f'{sanitize_name(nx_node.name)}'] # The tb will later parse this as the node name
619:             commands += [f'Current loaded ifmap: {current_loaded_ifmap_name}']
620: 
621:             print('============ Compiling Node ============')
622: 
623:             if u_code.mapped_node.depthwise:
624:                 print(f"Compiling {nx_node.name} as depthwise node...")
625:             
626:             if (u_code.inputs[0] != current_loaded_ifmap_name):
627:                 print(f"Compiling {nx_node.name} as first node of set...")
628:                 commands += [f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) reading ifmap ({u_code.inputs[0]}) from external memory']
629:             else:
630:                 print(f"Compiling {nx_node.name} as middle node of set...")
631:                 commands += [f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) will read ifmap ({u_code.inputs[0]}) from ACTMEM']
632: 
633:             if readout:
634:                 print(f"{nx_node.name} is followed by a non-compilable node, so it will write the ofmap to external memory.")
635:                 commands += [f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) will write ofmap ({u_code.outputs[0]}) into external memory']
636: 
637:             if u_code.mapped_node.bin_id is not None:
638:                 if u_code.mapped_node.bin_id != prev_bin_id:
639:                     print(f"Rewriting bin {nx_node.name} as bin changed from {prev_bin_id} to {u_code.mapped_node.bin_id}...")
640:                     commands += [f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) will rewrite the bin from {prev_bin_id} to {u_code.mapped_node.bin_id}']
641:                 else:
642:                     print(f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) BIN COMBO!!! Reusing bin {u_code.mapped_node.bin_id}.')
643:                     commands += [f'This node will reuse the bin {u_code.mapped_node.bin_id} from the previous node']
644:             else:
645:                 commands += [f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) is a depthwise node. Loaded bin ({prev_bin_id}) is preserved.']
646: 
647:             if preserve_ifmap:
648:                 commands += [f'NODE {nx_node.name} (id={u_code.mapped_node.node_id}) will preserve the ifmap ({u_code.inputs[0]}) in ACTMEM for the next node.']
649:                     
650:             # commands += [u_code.__repr__()]
651:             commands += ['ENDINFO']
652:             commands += u_code.compile(
653:                 include_ifmap_writes = (u_code.inputs[0] != current_loaded_ifmap_name) ,
654:                 write_weights        = u_code.mapped_node.bin_id                       != prev_bin_id,
655:                 add_read             = readout,
656:                 end                  = False ,
657:                 preserve_ifmap       = preserve_ifmap ,
658:             )
659: 
660:             prev_bin_id = u_code.mapped_node.bin_id if not u_code.mapped_node.depthwise else prev_bin_id  # If the node is depthwise, we don't change the bin id, as it will be the same as the previous node
661:         
662:             if not preserve_ifmap:
663:                 current_loaded_ifmap_name = u_code.outputs[0]
664: 
665:         else:
666:             print(f'Skipping {nx_node.name} as it is not compilable...')
667: 
668:     commands += ['END'] 
669:     prev_node = nx_node
670: 
671:     return commands
672: 
673: def get_info_command(u_code):
674: 
675:     """
676:     Generate an INFO command for the QRAcc testbench.
677:     This command is used to print information about the node.
678:     """
679:     commands = []
680:     commands += ['INFO']
681:     commands += [u_code.__repr__()]
682:     commands += ['ENDINFO']
683:     return commands
684: 
685: def sanitize_name(name: str) -> str:
686:     """Return a filesystem-safe version of *name* for use as a filename.
687: 
688:     Strips everything except alphanumerics, hyphens, and underscores,
689:     then resolves any remaining path tricks via ``pathlib``.
690:     """
691:     safe = name.replace(' ', '_')
692:     safe = ''.join(c for c in safe if c.isalnum() or c in '_-')
693:     # Use pathlib to collapse any residual traversal components
694:     safe = pathlib.PurePosixPath(safe).name
695:     return safe or 'node'

15:     def __init__(self,shape=None,precision=None,mode='symmetric',real_values=None,quantized_values=None,scale=None,zero_point=None):
16:         '''
17:         Quantized Tensor
18:         Reals are normalized to [-1,1]
19:         If mode is 'symmetric', zero point is 0 and scale is 2 / (2**precision - 1)
20: 
21:         Parameters:
22:         shape : tuple -- Shape of the tensor
23:         precision : int -- Number of bits to quantize to
24:         mode : str -- Quantization mode, 'symmetric', 'maxmin' or '3sigma'
25:         real_values : np.ndarray -- Real values of the tensor
26:         quantized_values : np.ndarray -- Quantized values of the tensor
27:         scale : float -- Scale of the quantization
28:         zero_point : float -- Zero point of the quantization
29:         
30:         Main attributes:
31:         real_values : np.ndarray -- Real values of the tensor
32:         quantized_values : np.ndarray -- Quantized values of the tensor
33:         scale : float -- Scale of the quantization
34:         zero_point : float -- Zero point of the quantization
35:         shape : tuple -- Shape of the tensor
36:         fake_quantized_values : np.ndarray -- Quantized values translated back to real range
37:         '''
38:         if real_values is not None:
39:             if precision is None:
40:                 raise ValueError('Precision must be provided')
41:             self.real_values = real_values
42:             self.quantize(precision,mode,zero_point=zero_point)
43:             self.dequantize()
44:         elif quantized_values is not None:
45:             if scale is None or zero_point is None:
46:                 raise ValueError('Scale and zero point must be provided')
47:             self.real_values = None
48:             self.quantized_values = quantized_values
49:             
50:             if type(scale) != np.ndarray:
51:                 self.scale = np.array([scale])
52:             else:
53:                 self.scale = scale
54: 
55:             self.zero_point = zero_point
56:             self.dequantize()
57:         else:
58:             if shape is None or precision is None:
59:                 raise ValueError('Shape and precision must be provided')
60:             self.real_values = np.random.uniform(-1,1,shape)
61:             self.quantize(precision,mode)
62:             self.dequantize()
63:         self.shape = self.real_values.shape
64:         return
65: 
66:     def quantize(self, precision, mode='symmetric', zero_point = None, signed=True):
67:         if mode == 'maxmin' : 
68:             clip_high = self.real_values.max()
69:             clip_low = self.real_values.min()
70:             # self.scale = 2*max(clip_high,clip_low) / (2**precision - 1)
71:             self.scale = 2*max(clip_high,clip_low) / (2**precision - 1)
72:             if zero_point is None:
73:                 if not signed:  
74:                     self.zero_point = 2 ** (precision - 1) - 1
75:                 self.zero_point = 0
76:             else:
77:                 self.zero_point = zero_point
78:         elif mode == '3sigma' :
79:             mean = self.real_values.mean()
80:             std = self.real_values.std()
81:             self.scale = std*3 / (2**precision - 1)
82:             if zero_point is None:
83:                 # if not signed: 
84:                 self.zero_point = 2 ** (precision - 1) - 1
85:                 # self.zero_point = mean / self.scale
86:             else:
87:                 self.zero_point = zero_point
88:         elif mode == 'symmetric':
89:             self.scale = 2 / (2**precision - 1)
90:             self.zero_point = 0    
91: 
92:         self.scale = np.array([self.scale]) 
93: 
94:         # Clipping
95:         if signed:
96:             clip_high = 2 ** (precision - 1) - 1
97:             clip_low = -2 ** (precision - 1)
98:         else:
99:             clip_high = 2 ** precision - 1
100:             clip_low = 0
101: 
102:         self.quantized_values = np.round( (self.real_values / self.scale) + self.zero_point ).astype(int)
103:         # self.quantized_values = np.clip(self.quantized_values, clip_low, clip_high)
104: 
105:     def dequantize(self):
106:         " Creates fake quantization values from the quantized values, like EdMIPS "
107:         fp_m = self.scale.reshape(self.scale.shape[0],*([1]*(len(self.quantized_values.shape)-1)))
108:         self.fake_quantized_values = (self.quantized_values - self.zero_point) * fp_m
109:         if self.real_values is None:
110:             self.real_values = self.fake_quantized_values
111:         return self.fake_quantized_values
112: 
113:     def __repr__(self):
114:         return self.quantized_values.__repr__()
115: 
116: def convolve_fake_quantized(a,w) -> np.ndarray:
117:     " o = aw "
118:     return convolve2d(a.fake_quantized_values,w.fake_quantized_values[::-1].T[::-1].T,mode='valid')
119: 
120: def convolve_reals(a,w) -> np.ndarray:
121:     " o = aw "
122:     return convolve2d(a.real_values,w.real_values[::-1].T[::-1].T,mode='valid')

124: def scaling_quantized_matmul(w,a,outBits,internalPrecision,out_scale = None) -> QuantizedTensor:
125:     " o = w @ a.T but quantized "
126: 
127:     # Matrix multiplication
128:     qaqw = w.quantized_values @ a.quantized_values.T
129: 
130:     # Scaling
131:     if out_scale is None:
132:         out_scale = 2 / (2**outBits - 1)
133:     newscale = a.scale * w.scale / out_scale
134:     m0, shift = convert_scale_to_shift_and_m0(
135:                     newscale,
136:                     precision=internalPrecision
137:                 )
138: 
139:     fp_m = m0 * 2**(shift)
140:     # m0bin = convert_to_fixed_point(m0, internalPrecision)
141:     # m0int = int(m0bin,base=2)
142: 
143:     scaled_clipped_shifted = (qaqw * fp_m).astype(int)
144:     o_q = saturating_clip(scaled_clipped_shifted, outBits)
145: 
146:     # Quantized tensor of the output

150:                     quantized_values=o_q,
151:                     scale=out_scale,
152:                     zero_point=0 # Assume 0 for now, might be bad later.
153:                 )
154: 
155:     return o_qtensor
156: 
157: def scaling_quantized_convolution(a,w,outBits,internalPrecision, out_scale = None) -> QuantizedTensor:
158:     " o = a * w but quantized "
159: 
160:     # Convolution
161:     qaqw = convolve2d(a.quantized_values,w.quantized_values[::-1].T[::-1].T,mode='valid')
162: 
163:     # Scaling
164:     if out_scale is None:
165:         out_scale = 2 / (2**outBits - 1)
166:     newscale = a.scale * w.scale / out_scale
167:     m0, shift = convert_scale_to_shift_and_m0(
168:                     newscale,
169:                     precision=internalPrecision
170:                 )
171: 
172:     fp_m = m0 * 2**(shift)
173: 
174:     # Reals accounting for quantized and fixed point error
175:     o_q = (qaqw * fp_m).astype(int)
176:     o_q = saturating_clip(o_q, outBits)
177: 
178:     # Quantized tensor of the output
179:     o_qtensor = QuantizedTensor(
180:                     qaqw.shape,

182:                     quantized_values=o_q,
183:                     scale=out_scale,

185:                 )
186: 
187:     return o_qtensor
188:     
189: def convert_scale_to_shift_and_m0(scale, precision=16):
190:     """Convert scale(s) to shift and zero point, vectorized."""
191:     if np.ndim(scale) == 0:
192:         if scale == 0:
193:             return np.float64(0.0), np.int64(0)
194:         shift = int(np.ceil(np.log2(scale)))
195:         m0 = scale / 2**shift
196:         mask_one = m0 == 1.0
197:         if mask_one:
198:             m0 = 0.9999999999999999
199:         x = int(np.floor(m0 * (2**precision)))
200:         m0 = np.float64(x) / (2**precision)
201:         return m0, np.int64(shift)
202:     
203:     scale = np.asarray(scale, dtype=np.float64)
204:     zero_mask = scale == 0
205:     shift = np.zeros_like(scale, dtype=np.int64)
206:     m0 = np.zeros_like(scale, dtype=np.float64)
207:     
208:     nonzero = ~zero_mask
209:     if np.any(nonzero):
210:         scale_nz = scale[nonzero]
211:         shift_vals = np.ceil(np.log2(scale_nz)).astype(np.int64)
212:         shift[nonzero] = shift_vals
213:         
214:         pow_shift = np.power(2.0, shift_vals.astype(np.float64))
215:         m0_raw = scale_nz / pow_shift
216:         
217:         mask_one = m0_raw == 1.0
218:         m0_raw_clamped = m0_raw.copy()
219:         m0_raw_clamped[mask_one] = 0.9999999999999999
220:         
221:         x = np.floor(m0_raw_clamped * (2**precision)).astype(np.int64)
222:         m0[nonzero] = x.astype(np.float64) / (2**precision)
223:     
224:     return m0, shift
225: 
226: vconvert_scale_to_shift_and_m0 = convert_scale_to_shift_and_m0

228: def convert_to_fixed_point(number, precision):
229:     """Convert a float (0,1) to fixed point binary string, vectorized."""
230:     number = np.asarray(number)
231:     # Handle number == 1 edge case (clamp to 0.999...)
232:     mask_one = number == 1
233:     number = number.copy()
234:     number[mask_one] = 0.9999999999999999
235:     
236:     # Compute integer representation
237:     x = np.floor(number * (2**precision)).astype(np.int64)
238:     
239:     # Vectorized binary representation using character operations
240:     result = np.empty(x.shape, dtype=f'U{precision}')
241:     for i in range(precision):
242:         # Extract the i-th bit from the left (MSB first)
243:         bit = ((x >> (precision - 1 - i)) & 1).astype(str)
244:         if i == 0:
245:             result[:] = bit  # Initialize with first bit
246:         else:
247:             result = np.char.add(result, bit)  # Append subsequent bits
248:     
249:     return result
250: 
251: def convert_to_fixed_point_int(number, precision):
252:     """Convert a float [0,1] to fixed point binary integer, vectorized."""
253:     if np.ndim(number) == 0:
254:         x = number * (2**precision)
255:         return int(np.floor(x))
256:     x = number * (2**precision)
257:     return np.floor(x).astype(np.int64)
258: 
259: vconvert_to_fixed_point_int = convert_to_fixed_point_int
260: 
261: def fixed_point_to_float(number, precision):
262:     """Convert a fixed point binary string to float [0,1], vectorized."""
263:     number = np.asarray(number)
264:     result = np.zeros(number.shape, dtype=np.float64)
265:     for i in range(precision):
266:         # Extract the i-th character from each string (vectorized)
267:         # We can't use np.char.slice (doesn't exist), so iterate over the
268:         # string array to build per-position character arrays.
269:         chars = np.array([s[i] for s in number.flat], dtype='U1').reshape(number.shape)
270:         bit = (chars == '1').astype(np.float64)
271:         result += bit * (2.0 ** -(i + 1))
272:     return result
273: 
274: def _right_shift(number, shift):
275:     """Right shift a number. Shifting rounds toward -infty, vectorized."""
276:     return np.floor(number / 2.**(shift)).astype(np.int64)
277: 
278: right_shift = _right_shift
279: 
280: def get_array_bits(array,signed=True):
281:     " Get the number of bits required to represent an array "
282:     return int(np.ceil(np.log2(np.abs(array).max())))+signed
283: 
284: def _saturating_clip (num_i, inBits=16, outBits=8, signed=True):
285:     '''
286:     Saturating clip, vectorized.
287: 
288:     This only implemented saturation before. Now it also clips.
289:     '''
290:     num_i_shifted = right_shift(num_i, inBits - outBits)
291: 
292:     if signed:
293:         min_val = -(2**(outBits-1))
294:         max_val = 2**(outBits-1)-1
295:     else:
296:         min_val = 0
297:         max_val = 2**outBits-1
298: 
299:     return np.clip(num_i_shifted, min_val, max_val)
300: 
301: saturating_clip = _saturating_clip
302: 
303: def binary_array_to_int(array,signed=False,outBits=None):
304:     ''' 
305:     Convert a binary array to an integer 
306:     The binary array is assumed to be in the last dimension
307:     [
308:     [ 0, 1, 1, 0],
309:     [ 1, 0, 0, 1],
310:     ...
311:     ]
312:     '''
313:     out = 0
314:     arr = np.moveaxis(array,-1,0)
315:     for bit in arr:
316:         out = (out << 1) | bit
317:     return out
318: 
319: def array_bin_to_int(array,signed=False,outBits=None):
320:     shape = array.shape
321:     pows = np.array([2**i for i in range(shape[-1])])[::-1]
322:     return np.sum(pows*array,axis=1)
323: 
324: def quantize_int8_to_bits(w_int8: np.ndarray, B: int, signed: bool) -> np.ndarray:
325:     """Reduce an int8 array to B bits (arithmetic shift), with clamping."""
326:     if B not in (1, 2, 4, 8):
327:         raise ValueError(f"B must be in {{1, 2, 4, 8}}, got {B}")
328:     if B == 8 and signed:
329:         return w_int8.astype(np.int64)
330:     if not signed:
331:         # unsigned only legal for B == 1; legacy binary weights are 0/1
332:         return (w_int8 != 0).astype(np.int64)
333:     q = w_int8.astype(np.int64) >> (8 - B)
334:     min_val = -(2 ** (B - 1))
335:     max_val = 2 ** (B - 1) - 1
336:     q = np.clip(q, min_val, max_val)
337:     return q
338: 
339: def weight_bit_planes(w_int8: np.ndarray, B: int, signed: bool) -> np.ndarray:
340:     """Return 8 bit-planes of shape (8,)+w_int8.shape. Plane b = (q >> b) & 1 for b < B, else 0."""
341:     q = quantize_int8_to_bits(w_int8, B, signed)
342:     planes = np.zeros((8,) + w_int8.shape, dtype=np.uint8)
343:     for b in range(B):
344:         planes[b] = ((q >> b) & 1).astype(np.uint8)
345:     return planes
346: 
347: def int_to_bin(n, bits):
348:     " Convert an integer to a binary array "
349:     return np.moveaxis(np.array([n >> i & 1 for i in range(bits-1,-1,-1)]),0,-1)
350: 
351: def int_to_trit(n, trits):
352:     " Convert an integer to a trit (ternary) array "
353:     a = int_to_bin(abs(n), trits)
354:     tritter = (((n < 0) * -2) + 1) # Bipolar array
355:     tritter = tritter.repeat(trits, axis=n.ndim-1).reshape(a.shape)
356:     return (a * tritter)
357: 
358: def simulate_trit_mul(w,x, trits, verbose=False, real=True):
359:     " trit-decomposed multiplication "
360:     x_trits = int_to_trit(x, trits)
361:     partials = x_trits.T @ w.T
362: 
363:     if(verbose):
364:         print('x trits:')
365:         print(df(x_trits))
366:         print('partials:')
367:         print(df(partials.T[::-1].T))
368: 
369:     if real:
370:         return po2_accumulate_real(partials, verbose=verbose)
371:     else:
372:         return po2_accumulate(partials, verbose=verbose)
373: 
374: def po2_accumulate(a,verbose=False):
375:     " Accumulate an array of powers of 2 "
376:     acc = np.zeros(a.shape[1])
377:     print('shifting accumulator:')
378:     for shift,row in enumerate(a[::-1]):
379:         acc = acc + row * 2**shift
380:         if verbose: print(acc)
381:     return acc
382: 
383: def po2_accumulate_real(a,verbose=False):
384:     " Accumulate an array of powers of 2 "
385:     acc = np.zeros(a.shape[1])
386:     print('shifting accumulator:')
387:     b = a * 2**(a.shape[0]-1)
388:     accs = []
389:     for shift,row in enumerate(b[::-1]):
390:         acc = acc/2
391:         acc = acc + row
392:         accs.append(acc.T[::-1].T.astype(int))
393:     if verbose: print(df(accs))
394:     return acc
395: 
396: def fprint_as_hex(
397:     filepath,
398:     array,
399:     elementBits = 8,
400:     wordSize = 32
401:     ):
402: 
403:     a = array.flatten()
404: 
405:     with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
406:         for i in range(0, len(a), wordSize // elementBits):
407:             hex_str = ''.join([f'{int(x):02x}' for x in a[i:i + wordSize // elementBits]])
408:             f.write(hex_str + '\n')
409:         f.write('\n')
410: 
411:     return
412: 
413: def as_packed_hex(
414:     array,
415:     elementBits = 8,
416:     wordSize = 32
417:     ):
418:     """
419:     Convert an array to a packed hex string.
420:     """
421:     a = array.flatten()
422:     chunk_size = wordSize // elementBits
423:     
424:     out = []
425:     for i in range(0, len(a), chunk_size):
426:         chunk = a[i:i + chunk_size]
427:         hex_str = ''.join([f'{int(x):02x}' for x in chunk])
428:         out.append(hex_str)
429:     
430:     return np.array(out, dtype=str)

20: def _hex3(n, hexits=8):
21:     """Format integer as hex, taking last hexits characters.
22:     
23:     Works on both scalars and numpy arrays.
24:     """
25:     if isinstance(n, np.ndarray):
26:         # Vectorized path for arrays - use list comprehension for speed
27:         arr = np.asarray(n, dtype=np.uint32)
28:         return np.array([_hex3(x, hexits) for x in arr.flat]).reshape(arr.shape)
29:     # Scalar path
30:     return f"{(int(n) & 0xffffffff):08x}"[-hexits:]

34: # vhex3 and hex_but_no_0x are now proper functions
35: vhex3 = _hex3  # Keep backward compatibility; _hex3 handles both scalars and arrays via isinstance check
36: hex_but_no_0x = _hex_but_no_0x

38: def kernel_to_writes(kernel, channels, hexes=True):
39:     '''
40:     Packs every 4 elements of the kernel channel dimension (in HWC) into a single 32-bit integer
41:     Pads with zeros if the flattened kernel is not a multiple of 4.
42: 
43:     If hexes is True, returns a list of hex strings, otherwise returns a numpy array of integers.
44:     ''' 
45:     kernel_flat = kernel.reshape(-1, channels)
46:     kernel_flat = np.pad(kernel_flat, ((0, 0), (0, 4 - (kernel_flat.shape[1] % 4) if kernel_flat.shape[1] % 4 != 0 else 0)), mode='constant', constant_values=0)
47:     kernel_flat = kernel_flat.reshape(-1, 4).astype(np.uint8)
48:     # Build the packed word from Python ints: shifting uint8 values overflows
49:     # and zeroes the high bytes, so the non-hex path must not shift on uint8.
50:     kernel_int = kernel_flat.astype(np.int64) & 0xFF
51:     if hexes:
52:         kernel_packed = vhex3(kernel_flat, hexits=2)
53:         kernel_writes = []
54:     else:
55:         kernel_writes = np.zeros(kernel_flat.shape[0], dtype=np.uint32)
56:     for i in range(kernel_flat.shape[0]):
57:         if hexes:
58:             kernel_writes.append(f'{kernel_packed[i,3]}{kernel_packed[i,2]}{kernel_packed[i,1]}{kernel_packed[i,0]}')
59:         else:
60:             word = int(kernel_int[i,3]) << 24 | int(kernel_int[i,2]) << 16 | int(kernel_int[i,1]) << 8 | int(kernel_int[i,0])
61:             kernel_writes[i] = np.uint32(word)
62:     return kernel_writes

64: def _conv2d_int(x, w, padding=0, stride=1):
65:     x = np.pad(x.astype(np.int64), ((0,0),(0,0),(padding,padding),(padding,padding)))
66:     w = w.astype(np.int64)
67:     n, _, h, wd = x.shape
68:     o, _, kh, kw = w.shape
69:     oh = (h - kh) // stride + 1
70:     ow = (wd - kw) // stride + 1
71:     out = np.zeros((n, o, oh, ow), dtype=np.int64)
72:     for i in range(oh):
73:         for j in range(ow):
74:             patch = x[:, :, i*stride:i*stride+kh, j*stride:j*stride+kw]
75:             out[:, :, i, j] = np.einsum('ncij,ocij->no', patch, w)
76:     return out.astype(np.int32)

78: def generate_random_torch_conv(
79:     ifmap_shape,
80:     ifmap_bits,
81:     kernel_shape,
82:     kernel_bits,
83:     stride,
84:     seed = 0
85: ):
86:     
87:     t_ifmap = np.random.randint(0, 2**(ifmap_bits), size=ifmap_shape).astype(np.int32)
88:     t_kern = np.random.randint(0, 2**(kernel_bits), size=kernel_shape).astype(np.int32)
89:     t_res = _conv2d_int(t_ifmap, t_kern, padding=1, stride=stride)
90: 
91:     t_matrix = t_kern.transpose(0, 2, 3, 1)
92:     t_matrix = t_matrix.reshape(t_kern.shape[0],-1).T  
93: 
94:     t_toeplitz = compute.toeplitzize_input(
95:         in_tensor=t_ifmap.squeeze(0), 
96:         ksize=kernel_shape[-1], 
97:         strides=stride,
98:         channel_minor=True
99:     )
100: 
101:     a = t_toeplitz @ t_matrix
102:     output_shape = (
103:         ifmap_shape[0],
104:         kernel_shape[0],
105:         ifmap_shape[2]//stride, 
106:         ifmap_shape[3]//stride
107:     )
108:     out = a.T.reshape(*output_shape).astype(int)
109: 
110:     return t_res, t_matrix, t_ifmap, t_toeplitz, out

115:         return np.random.rand(*shape)

134:     if acts_mode == 'random':

167:     ifmap_shape,

173:     Infer the output shape of a convolution (or matmul) operation.

178:         ifmap_shape[0],
179:         kernel_shape[0],

297: def infer_optimal_adc_range_shifts(tplitz_act_vector, weights, ifmap_bits = 8):
298:     first_partials = quant.int_to_trit(tplitz_act_vector,ifmap_bits).T @ weights
299:     mean_partial = first_partials.mean()
300:     if mean_partial < 1:
301:         adc_ref_range_shifts = 0
302:     adc_ref_range_shifts = np.ceil(np.log(mean_partial)/np.log(2)) - 3
303:     if adc_ref_range_shifts < 0:
304:         adc_ref_range_shifts = 0
305:     if np.isnan(adc_ref_range_shifts):
306:         adc_ref_range_shifts = 0
307: 
308:     return adc_ref_range_shifts

310: def minorize_pad_ifmap(t_ifmap, padding=1, act_zero_point=0):
311:     """
312:     Prepare the input feature map for convolution by padding it.
313:     """
314:     zp_x = float(act_zero_point)
315:     ifmap_channel_minor = np.pad(t_ifmap, ((0,0),(0,0),(padding,padding),(padding,padding)), mode='constant', constant_values=zp_x)
316:     ifmap_channel_minor = ifmap_channel_minor.transpose(0, 2, 3, 1)
317:     return ifmap_channel_minor

319: def pack_ifmap_to_ints(t_ifmap):
320:     """
321:     Pack the input feature map into integers.
322:     """
323:     ifmap_channel_packed_ints = [int(i,base=16) for i in quant.as_packed_hex(t_ifmap)]
324:     ifmap_channel_packed_ints = np.array(ifmap_channel_packed_ints)
325:     return ifmap_channel_packed_ints
326: 
327: def pad_scaler_writes(scale_to_map,core_shape,output_zero_point,mm_offset_x):
328:     if scale_to_map.ndim == 0:
329:         scale_to_map = np.repeat(scale_to_map, core_shape[1])
330:     scale = np.ones((core_shape[1],), dtype=np.float32)
331:     scales_width = scale_to_map.shape[0]
332:     scale[mm_offset_x:mm_offset_x+scales_width] = scale_to_map
333:     m0, shift = quant.vconvert_scale_to_shift_and_m0(scale, precision=16)
334:     int_scale = quant.vconvert_to_fixed_point_int(m0,16)
335:     scaler_data = output_zero_point.astype(int) * (2**20) + int_scale * (2**4) + (-shift)
336:     return scaler_data
337: 
338: def pad_bias_data(biases_to_map, core_shape, mm_offset_x):
339:     biases = np.zeros((core_shape[1],), dtype=np.int32)
340:     biases_width = biases_to_map.shape[0]
341:     biases[mm_offset_x:mm_offset_x+biases_width] = biases_to_map
342:     return biases
343: 
344: def imc_matrix_to_writes(t_matrix, core_shape, x_offset, y_offset, num_bank_cols = 32):
345:     matrix_map = map_single_matrix(t_matrix, core_shape, x_offset=x_offset, y_offset=y_offset)
346:     write_array = mapped_matrix_to_bank_writes(matrix_map,num_bank_cols)
347:     return write_array
348: 
349: def write_weight_rows_to_asm(write_array: np.ndarray | list, address: str = DATA_WRITE_ADDR, words_per_row: int = 8, signed: bool = False) -> list[str]:
350:     """Emit LOADs for multibit weight rows: words_per_row LOADs per row.
351:     Address = DATA_WRITE_ADDR | (row << log2(words_per_row)) | word.
352:     Word w holds row bits [w*hostBusBits +: hostBusBits] (plane w of the row).
353:     Legacy binary emitter (write_array_to_asm) is unchanged."""
354:     asm = []
355:     base_addr = int(address, 16)
356:     row_shift = (words_per_row - 1).bit_length()
357:     for idx, element in enumerate(write_array):
358:         row = idx // words_per_row
359:         word = idx % words_per_row
360:         addr = base_addr | (row << row_shift) | word
361:         val = int(element)
362:         if signed:
363:             # Encode each weight byte as two's complement (int64 & 0xFF, like kernel_to_writes)
364:             b0 = int(np.int64(val) & 0xFF)
365:             b1 = int(np.int64(val) >> 8 & 0xFF)
366:             b2 = int(np.int64(val) >> 16 & 0xFF)
367:             b3 = int(np.int64(val) >> 24 & 0xFF)
368:             val = (b3 << 24) | (b2 << 16) | (b1 << 8) | b0
369:         asm.append(f"LOAD {addr:08x} {vhex3(val)}")
370:     return asm
371: 
372: def write_input_files(res_dict, savepath):
373:     if savepath is not None:
374:         os.makedirs(savepath, exist_ok=True)
375:         for key, value in res_dict.items():
376:             if type(value) is dict:
377:                 continue
378:             if value.dtype in ['int32','float64']:
379:                 np.savetxt(f'{savepath}/{key}.txt', value.flatten(), fmt='%d')
380:             else:
381:                 np.savetxt(f'{savepath}/{key}.txt', value.flatten(), fmt='%s')
382:             np.savetxt(f'{savepath}/{key}_shape.txt', value.shape, fmt='%d')
383: 
384: 
385: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
386: # Legacy functions below are retained for backward compatibility but are NOT
387: # used by the main MARP pipeline.  They reference an older API of
388: # ``sample_onnx_qlinearconv`` and may not work without adaptation.
389: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
390: 
391:

396:     ifmap_bits,
397:     kernel_shape,

402:     mm_offset_x = 0,

409:     t_res, t_matrix, t_ifmap, t_toeplitz, scaler_params = sample_onnx_qlinearconv(

412:         kernel_shape=kernel_shape,
413:         kernel_bits=kernel_bits,
414:         kernel_dtype = np.int8,
415:         pads = (padding,padding,padding,padding),
416:         stride = stride,
417:         seed = seed,
418:         depthwise = depthwise,
419:         # weight_density=0.05,
420:         # acts_mode='counting',
421:         # weight_mode='spatial'
422:     )
423: 
424:     if not depthwise:
425:         write_array = imc_matrix_to_writes(t_matrix, core_shape, x_offset=mm_offset_x, y_offset=mm_offset_y)
426:     else:
427:         kernel = t_matrix
428:         kernel_hwc = kernel.transpose(1,2,3,0)
429:         write_array = kernel_to_writes(kernel_hwc, DWC_CORE_SIZE, hexes=False)
430: 
431:     scaler_data = pad_scaler_writes(scaler_params['scale'], 
432:                                         core_shape=core_shape,
433:                                         output_zero_point=scaler_params['output_zp'],
434:                                         mm_offset_x=mm_offset_x)
435:     bias_data = pad_bias_data(scaler_params['gph_node'][0].biases, 
436:                                         core_shape=core_shape, 
437:                                         mm_offset_x=mm_offset_x)
438:     
439:     loaded_ifmap_padding = padding if soft_padding else 0 
440: 
441:     minorized_padded_ifmap = minorize_pad_ifmap(t_ifmap, padding=loaded_ifmap_padding ,act_zero_point = scaler_params['zp_x'])
442: 
443:     if soft_padding:
444:         print(f'[STIM_GEN] Using SOFT padding with {loaded_ifmap_padding} pixels. IFMAP shape: {minorized_padded_ifmap.shape}')
445:     else:
446:         print(f'[STIM_GEN] Using HARDWARE padding. IFMAP shape: {minorized_padded_ifmap.shape}')
447: 
448:     raw_data = {
449:         'ifmap':pack_ifmap_to_ints(minorized_padded_ifmap),
450:         'biases':bias_data,
451:         'scales':scaler_data,
452:         'weights':write_array,
453:     }

457:         'toeplitz': t_toeplitz,

460:         'scaler_data': scaler_data,
461:         'biases': bias_data,
462:         'scaler_params' : scaler_params # Can be removed later
463:     }
464:     write_input_files(res_dict, savepath)
465: 
466:     return raw_data, res_dict

468: def generate_top_inputs(
469:     savepath,
470:     stride,
471:     ifmap_shape,
472:     ifmap_bits,
473:     kernel_shape,
474:     kernel_bits,
475:     core_shape,
476:     depthwise = False,
477:     padding = 1,

479:     mm_offset_y = 0,
480:     seed = 0

483:     np.random.seed(seed)

485:     print("[STIM_GEN] Generating Sample QLinearConv")
486:     print(f"t_res, t_matrix, t_ifmap, t_toeplitz, scaler_params = sample_onnx_qlinearconv(ifmap_shape={ifmap_shape},ifmap_bits={ifmap_bits},kernel_shape={kernel_shape},kernel_bits={kernel_bits},kernel_dtype = np.int8,pads = (1,1,1,1),stride = {stride},seed = {seed})")
487:

491:         kernel_shape=kernel_shape,
492:         kernel_bits=kernel_bits,
493:         kernel_dtype = np.int8,

497:         depthwise = depthwise,

500:         # weight_mode='spatial'
501:     )

504:     print(f'matrix_map = map_single_matrix({t_matrix}, {core_shape}, x_offset={mm_offset_x}, y_offset={mm_offset_y})')
505:     matrix_map = map_single_matrix(t_matrix, core_shape, x_offset=mm_offset_x, y_offset=mm_offset_y)
506:     write_array = mapped_matrix_to_bank_writes(matrix_map, NUM_BANK_COLS)
507: 
508:     # Software padding and channel minor
509:     zp_x = float(scaler_params['zp_x'])
510:     ifmap_channel_minor = np.pad(t_ifmap, ((0,0),(0,0),(padding,padding),(padding,padding)), mode='constant', constant_values=zp_x)
511:     ifmap_channel_minor = ifmap_channel_minor.transpose(0, 2, 3, 1)
512: 
513:     # Press activations into 32b words of 4 elements each

529:     scaler_data = scaler_data

534:     biases[mm_offset_x:mm_offset_x+scaler_params['gph_node'][0].biases.shape[0]] = biases_to_map
535:

540:         'result': t_res,

542:         'ifmap': ifmap_channel_packed_ints,
543:         'ifmap_ints': ifmap_channel_minor,
544:         'small_matrix': t_matrix,

547:         'scaler_data': scaler_data,
548:         'biases': biases,
549:     }

551:     if savepath is not None:
552:         for key, value in res_dict.items():

555:             else:

561: 
562: def generate_qracc_inputs(
563:     wDimX = 8,
564:     wDimY = 128,
565:     xBatches = 50,
566:     xTrits = 4,
567:     outBits = 4,
568:     seed = 0,
569:     weight_mode = 'bipolar',
570:     col_symmetric = False,
571:     rangeBits = None,
572:     x_repeat = False,
573:     clip_output = True,
574:     unsigned_acts = False,
575:     bitRange = None
576: ):
577:     '''
578:     Generates integer weights and inputs, including clipped integer reference outputs.
579: 
580:     Parameters:
581:     wDimX : int -- Number of columns
582:     wDimY : int -- Number of rows
583:     xBatches : int -- Number of batches
584:     xTrits : int -- Number of trits for input data
585:     outBits : int -- Number of bits for output data
586:     seed : int -- Random seed
587:     weight_mode : str -- 'binary' or 'bipolar'
588:     col_symmetric : bool -- If True, all columns will have the same output value
589:     '''
590:     np.random.seed(seed)
591:     wShape = (wDimX, wDimY)
592:     xShape = (xBatches, wDimY)
593: 
594:     if col_symmetric:
595:         # In this mode, every column will have the same output value
596:         print('[STIM_GEN] Generating symmetric columns')
597:         if weight_mode=='binary':
598:             w = np.random.randint(0,2, wDimY)
599:             w = w.repeat(wDimX).reshape(wDimY, wDimX).T
600:         elif weight_mode=='bipolar':
601:             w = np.random.randint(0,2, wShape)*2-1
602:             w = w.repeat(wDimX).reshape(wDimY, wDimX).T
603:         else:
604:             raise ValueError('Invalid weight_mode')
605:     else:
606:         print('[STIM_GEN] Generating random weights')
607:         if weight_mode=='binary':
608:             # When doing unsigned acts with 1b binary weights, the output tends to oversaturate. We need to make the weights sparse to have testable outputs.
609:             w = np.random.randint(0,2, wShape)
610:             if unsigned_acts:
611:                 a = np.random.rand(*wShape)
612:                 w = (a < 0.1).astype(int) # Sparse Weights
613:         elif weight_mode=='bipolar':
614:             w = np.random.randint(0,2, wShape)*2-1
615:         else:
616:             raise ValueError('Invalid weight_mode')
617: 
618:     if bitRange is None:
619:         bitRange = xTrits
620:     if not unsigned_acts:
621:         low_lim = -(2**(bitRange)-1)
622:         high_lim = 2**(bitRange)
623:     else:
624:         low_lim = 0
625:         high_lim = 2**(bitRange)
626: 
627:     if x_repeat:
628:         x = np.random.randint(low_lim, high_lim,wDimY)
629:         x = x.repeat(xBatches).reshape(wDimY, xBatches).T
630:     else:
631:         x = np.random.randint(low_lim, high_lim,xShape)
632:     wx = w @ x.T
633: 
634:     # Get_array_bits is incorrect. The real bit count depends on the range of the input data
635:     # wxBits = quant.get_array_bits(wx)
636: 
637:     if rangeBits is None:
638:         wxBits = np.log2(wDimY) + 1 # +1 because of bipolar rep (-128,128) vs (0,128)
639:     else:
640:         wxBits = rangeBits
641: 
642:     if clip_output:
643:         wx_outBits = quant.saturating_clip(wx, inBits = wxBits, outBits = outBits)
644:     else:
645:         wx_outBits = wx
646:     return w, x, wx_outBits.T

---

184:     .qrAccInputBits                 (qrAccInputBits),
185:     .qrAccInputElements             (qrAccInputElements),
186:     .qrAccOutputBits                (qrAccOutputBits),
187:     .qrAccOutputElements            (qrAccOutputElements),
188:     .qrAccAdcBits                   (qrAccAdcBits),
189:     .qrAccAccumulatorBits           (qrAccAccumulatorBits),
190: 
191:     .numRows                        (numRows),
192:     .numCols                        (numCols),
193:     .numBanks                       (numBanks),
194: 
195:     .globalBufferDepth              (globalBufferDepth),
196:     .globalBufferExtInterfaceWidth  (globalBufferExtInterfaceWidth),
197:     .globalBufferIntInterfaceWidth  (globalBufferIntInterfaceWidth),
198:     .globalBufferAddrWidth          (globalBufferAddrWidth),
199:     .globalBufferDataSize           (globalBufferDataSize),
200: 
201:     .aflDimY                        (aflDimY),
202:     .aflDimX                        (aflDimX),
203:     .aflAddrWidth                   (aflAddrWidth)
204:     `endif
205: ) u_qr_acc_top (
206:     .clk                            (clk),
207:     .nrst                           (nrst),
208: 
209:     // Control Interface
210:     .bus_req_i                      (bus_req),
211:     .bus_resp_o                     (bus_resp),
212: 
213:     // Analog passthrough signals
214:     `ifdef POST_SYNTH
215:     .to_analog                      (to_analog_wire),
216:     .from_analog                    (from_analog_wire),
217:     `else
218:     .to_analog                      (to_analog),
219:     .from_analog                    (from_analog),
220:     `endif 
221: 
222:     .bank_select                    (bank_select)
223: );
224: 
225: qracc_csr #(
226:     .csrWidth                   (32),
227:     .numCsr                     (16)
228: ) u_csr_tb_model (
229:     .clk                        (clk),
230:     .nrst                       (nrst),
231:     .bus_req_i                  (bus_req),
232:     .bus_resp_o                 (),

237:     .csr_main_busy              (0),
238:     .csr_main_inst_write_mode   (),
239:     .csr_main_internal_state    (0)

282: // Fake L2 Memory
283: 
284: logic [7:0] l2_mem [2**21-1];
285: logic [31:0] l2_mem_addr;
286: logic l2_mem_enable;
287: always_ff @( posedge clk or negedge nrst ) begin : l2Mem
288:     
289:     if(!nrst) l2_mem_addr <= 0;
290:     
291:     if (bus_resp.rd_data_valid && l2_mem_enable) begin
292:         for (int i = 0; i < 32/8; i++) begin
293:             l2_mem[l2_mem_addr + i] <= bus_resp.data_out[(32/8-1-i)*8 +: 8];
294:         end
295:         l2_mem_addr <= l2_mem_addr + 4;
296:     end
297: end

348:     "scaler_data",

358:     foreach (files[i]) begin
359:         $display("%d: %s",i,files[i]);
360:         file_descriptors[i] = $fopen({files_path,files[i],".txt"},"r");

369: function int get_index_of_file(input string file_name);
370:     int i;
371:     foreach (files[i]) begin
372:         if (files[i] == file_name) begin
373:             return i;
374:         end
375:     end
376: endfunction
377: 
378: function int input_files(input string file_name);
379:     // $display("Opening file %s at %d",file_name,get_index_of_file(file_name));
380:     return file_descriptors[get_index_of_file(file_name)];
381: endfunction 
382: 
383: `ifndef VERILATOR
384: class NumpyArray;
385: 
386:     int array [];
387:     int shape [];
388:     int size = 1;
389:     string file_name;
390: 
391:     function new (input string file_name);
392:         this.file_name = file_name;
393:         read_shape(file_name);
394:         for (int i=0;i<this.shape.size();i++) begin
395:             this.size *= this.shape[i];
396:         end
397:         this.array = new[this.size];
398:         read_array(file_name);
399:         // $display("==== Array ====");
400:         // $display("%s: %d",file_name,this.size);
401:         // $display("Shape: ");
402:         // for (int i=0;i<this.shape.size();i++) begin
403:         //     $display("%d",this.shape[i]);
404:         // end
405:     endfunction
406: 
407:     function void read_shape(input string file_name);
408:         int fd;
409:         fd = input_files({file_name,"_shape"});
410:         for (int i=0;!$feof(fd);i++) begin
411:             this.shape = new[this.shape.size()+1] (this.shape);
412:             $fscanf(fd,"%d",this.shape[i]);
413:         end
414:         this.shape = new[this.shape.size()-1] (this.shape);
415:         void'($fseek(fd,0,0));
416:     endfunction
417: 
418:     function void read_array(input string file_name);
419:         int fd;
420:         fd = input_files(file_name);
421:         void'($fseek(fd,0,0));
422:         for (int i=0;!$feof(fd);i++) begin
423:             $fscanf(fd,"%d",this.array[i]);
424:         end
425:         void'($fseek(fd,0,0));
426:     endfunction
427: 
428:     function void print_shape();
429:         $display("Shape: ");
430:         for (int i=0;i<this.shape.size();i++) begin
431:             $display("%d",this.shape[i]);
432:         end
433:     endfunction
434: 
435:     function void print_array();
436:         $display("Array: ");
437:         for (int i=0;i<this.size;i++) begin
438:             $write("%d\t",this.array[i]);
439:         end
440:         $write("\n");
441:     endfunction
442: 
443:     function int index (
444:         input int idx[]
445:     );
446:         // Accesses array indices like array.index( {3,2,1 } ) similar to numpy array[3][2][1]
447: 
448:         int flat_index;
449:         flat_index = 0;
450:         // $write("[ARRAY %s] Accessing indices ", this.file_name);
451:         for (int i=0;i<this.shape.size();i++) begin
452:             // $write("%d ",idx[i]);
453:             if (i == 0)
454:                 flat_index = idx[i];
455:             else
456:                 flat_index = flat_index * this.shape[i] + idx[i];
457:         end
458:         // $write("\t Flat index of %d\n", flat_index);
459:         if (flat_index < 0) begin
460:             $display("[ARRAY] Negative index: %d", flat_index);
461:             return -1;
462:         end
463:         
464:         // if (flat_index >= this.size) begin
465:         //     $display("[ARRAY] Index out of bounds: %d", flat_index);
466:         //     return -1;
467:         // end
468:         return this.array[flat_index];
469:     endfunction
470: 
471: endclass
472: `endif // VERILATOR

508:     $display("mapped_matrix_offset_y: %d", u_qr_acc_top.cfg.mapped_matrix_offset_y);

510:     $display("padding : %d", u_qr_acc_top.cfg.padding);

515: task status_checkup();
516:     $display("STATE: %s", u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.state_q.name());
517: endtask
518: 
519: localparam MAX_TROWS = 10000;
520: 
521: task track_toeplitz();
522: 
523:     int trow, tplitz_offset, tplitz_height, reference;
524:     logic errflag;
525: 
526: 
527:     tb_state = S_COMPUTE;
528:     errflag = 0;
529:     trow = 0;
530:     tplitz_offset = {16'b0,cfg.mapped_matrix_offset_y};
531:     tplitz_height = cfg.filter_size_y * cfg.filter_size_x * cfg.num_input_channels;
532: 
533:     // Track only during compute state
534:     while( (u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.state_q == u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.state_d) ) begin
535: 
536:         if (trow >= MAX_TROWS) begin
537:             $display("ERROR: Reached maximum number of rows (%d). Stopping tracking.", MAX_TROWS);
538:             $finish;
539:         end
540: 
541:         // Track only when the MAC input is accepted by the seq_acc OR the wsacc
542:         if((u_qr_acc_top.qracc_ctrl.qracc_mac_data_valid && u_qr_acc_top.qracc_ready) || u_qr_acc_top.qracc_ctrl.wsacc_data_i_valid) begin
543:             $write("Window [%d][%d,%d]: \n", trow, u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.opix_pos_x, u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.opix_pos_y);
544:             
545:             // Produces a -- pattern for irrelevant activations
546:             for (j=0;j<288;j++) begin
547:                 if (j >= tplitz_offset && j < tplitz_offset + tplitz_height) begin
548:                     reference  = toeplitz.index( {trow,j-tplitz_offset} );
549:                     $write("%h",u_qr_acc_top.feature_loader_data_out[j]);
550:                     if (u_qr_acc_top.feature_loader_data_out[j] != reference[7:0]) begin
551:                         $write("!");
552:                         errflag = 1;
553:                         errcnt++;
554:                     end else begin
555:                         $write(" ");
556:                     end
557:                 end else begin
558:                     $write("-- ");
559:                 end
560:                 if (j % 32 == 31) begin
561:                     $write("\n");
562:                 end
563:             end
564:             $write("\n");
565: 
566:             // If error, print the reference 
567:             if (errflag) begin
568:                 $write("===================== WRONG ====================: at time %d\n",$time);
569:                 for (j=0;j<288;j++) begin
570:                     if (j >= tplitz_offset && j < tplitz_offset + tplitz_height) begin
571:                         reference  = toeplitz.index( {trow,j-tplitz_offset} );
572:                         $write("%h ",reference[7:0]);
573:                     end else begin
574:                         $write("-- ");
575:                     end
576:                     if (j % 32 == 31) begin
577:                         $write("\n");
578:                     end
579:                 end
580:                 $write("\n");
581:                 errflag=0;
582:             end
583:             trow++;
584:         end
585:         #(CLK_PERIOD);
586:     end
587:     #(CLK_PERIOD);
588: endtask
589: 
590: task check_scales();
591:     logic [15:0] scale;
592:     logic [3:0] shift;
593:     logic signed [31:0] bias;
594:     logic [15:0] scaleref;
595:     logic [3:0] shiftref;
596:     logic signed [31:0] biasref;
597:     $display("Checking scales at time %t", $time);
598: 
599:     for (i=0;i<scaler_data.size;i++) begin
600:         // if (i%4 == 0) begin
601:         //     $write("\n");
602:         //     $write("[%d]:\t",i);
603:         // end else begin
604:         //     $write("\t");
605:         // end
606:         $write("[%d]:\t",i);
607:         scale = u_qr_acc_top.u_output_scaler_set.output_scale[i];
608:         shift = u_qr_acc_top.u_output_scaler_set.output_shift[i];
609:         bias = u_qr_acc_top.u_output_scaler_set.output_bias[i];
610:         
611:         scaleref = scaler_data.array[i][4+:16];
612:         shiftref = scaler_data.array[i][0+:4];
613:         biasref = biases.array[i];
614:         $write("%d\t, %d\t, %d\t",scale,shift,bias);
615:         if (scale != scaleref | shift != shiftref | bias != biasref) begin
616:             $write(" != %d, %d, %d",scaleref, shiftref, biasref);
617:             errcnt++;
618:         end
619:         $write("\n");
620:     end
621:     $write("\n");
622: 
623: endtask
624: 
625: `ifdef MODEL_MEM
626: task magic_export_ofmap();
627: 
628:     // Magically export of the ofmap without actual readout
629:     input string output_file_name;
630: 
631:     int a;
632:     int fd;
633:     int ofmap_size;
634: 
635:     ofmap_size = cfg.output_fmap_dimx * cfg.output_fmap_dimy * cfg.num_output_channels;
636: 
637:     $display("Exporting ofmap to file %s at time %t", {output_path,output_file_name,".txt"}, $time);
638: 
639:     fd = $fopen({output_path,output_file_name,".txt"},"w");
640:     if (fd == 0) begin
641:         $display("[TB ERROR] cannot create output file %s", {output_path,output_file_name,".txt"});
642:         $fatal(1, "cannot create output file");
643:     end
644:     for(i=0;i<ofmap_size;i++) begin
645:         // Later on this would be wrong if the output isn't 8b
646:         // and the 4b or 2b version is packed.
647:         if (cfg.unsigned_acts) begin
648:             a = u_qr_acc_top.u_activation_buffer.mem[u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.ifmap_start_addr + i];
649:         end else begin
650:             a = $signed(u_qr_acc_top.u_activation_buffer.mem[u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.ifmap_start_addr + i]);
651:         end
652:         $fwrite(fd,"%d\n",a);
653:     end
654: 
655: endtask
656: 
657: // Address by 4-byte word
658: task check_acts();
659:     int ptr;
660:     int word;
661:     ptr = u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.ifmap_start_addr;
662: 
663:     $display("Checking activations at time %t", $time);
664:     for (i=0;i<ifmap.size*4;i=i+4) begin
665:         if (i%16 == 0) begin
666:             $write("\n");
667:             $write("[%d]:\t",i);
668:         end else begin
669:             $write("\t");
670:         end
671:         
672:         word = {u_qr_acc_top.u_activation_buffer.mem[ptr + i], 
673:                 u_qr_acc_top.u_activation_buffer.mem[ptr + i + 1],
674:                 u_qr_acc_top.u_activation_buffer.mem[ptr + i + 2],
675:                 u_qr_acc_top.u_activation_buffer.mem[ptr + i + 3]};
676:         
677:         $write("%h\t",word);
678:         if (word != ifmap.array[i >> 2]) begin
679:             $write(" != %h",ifmap.array[i >> 2]);
680:             errcnt++;
681:         end
682:     end
683:     $write("\n");
684: endtask
685: 
686: `endif // MODEL_MEM
687: `endif // VERILATOR
688:

690: 
691:     int a;
692:     int fd;
693:     int ofmap_size;
694: 
695:     ofmap_size = cfg.output_fmap_dimx * cfg.output_fmap_dimy * cfg.num_output_channels;
696: 
697:     $display("Exporting ofmap at time %t", $time);
698:

700:     if (fd == 0) begin

702:         $fatal(1, "cannot create output file");

708: endtask

710: `endif // POST_SYNTH

712: task start_sim();
713: 
714:     // errcnt = 0;
715: 
716:     // SRAM_COLS must be multiple of 32
717:     if (qrAccInputElements % 32 != 0) begin
718:         $display("Error: SRAM_COLS must be multiple of 32");
719:         $finish;
720:     end
721: 
722:     bus_req.wen = 0;
723:     bus_req.valid = 0;
724:     bus_req.addr = 0;
725:     bus_req.data_in = 0;
726: 
727:     $display("=============== STARTING SIMULATION ===============");
728:     nrst = 0;
729:     #(CLK_PERIOD*2);
730:     nrst = 1;
731:     #(CLK_PERIOD*2);
732: endtask
733: 
734: task bus_write_loop();
735: 
736:     int fd;
737:     int data;
738:     int addr;
739:     int i;
740:     string node_name;
741:     qracc_trigger_t trigger_type;
742:     string command;
743:     fd = $fopen({files_path,"commands.txt"},"r");
744:     
745:     $display("=========== BUS COMMAND LOOP ============");
746:     if (fd == 0) begin
747:         $display("Error opening commands file");
748:         $finish;
749:     end
750: 
751:     while (!$feof(fd)) begin
752:         $fscanf(fd,"%s", command);
753:         case(command)
754:             "INFO": begin
755:                 `ifdef TRACK_STATISTICS
756:                 // Save statistics of the previous node
757:                 if (node_name != "") begin
758:                     append_stats_to_csv({output_path,"qracc_statistics",".csv"},node_name);
759:                     reset_statistics();
760:                 end
761:                 `endif
762:                 $display("=== NODE INFORMATION ===");
763:                 $fscanf(fd,"\n%s", node_name);
764:                 // $fgets(node_name, fd);
765:                 $display("Node Name: %s", node_name);
766:                 do begin
767:                     $fgets(command, fd);
768:                     $write("%s ", command);
769:                 end while (command != "ENDINFO\n");
770:                 $display("=== END OF NODE INFORMATION ===");
771:             end
772:             "LOAD": begin
773:                 $fscanf(fd,"%h %h",addr,data);
774: 
775:                 trigger_type = qracc_trigger_t'(data[2:0]);
776: 
777:                 if ((addr & 32'hFFFF_FFF0)== 32'h0000_0010) begin
778:                     if (data[2:0] != TRIGGER_IDLE) begin
779:                         $display("Triggering QRAcc with command: %s", trigger_type.name());
780:                     end
781:                 end
782:                 if (addr == 32'h0000_0011) begin
783:                     adc_ref_range_shifts = data[7:4];
784:                 end
785:

917:
