919: `ifdef SYNOPSYS
920: 
921: // SAIF tracking variables
922: logic [31:0] saif_state_counter = 0;
923: logic [31:0] previous_state;
924: logic [31:0] current_state;
925: logic saif_initialized = 0;
926: string saif_filename;
927: 
928: // Task to start SAIF recording for a specific state
929: task start_saif_recording(input string state_name);
930:     saif_filename = $sformatf("%s/saif_%s_%0d.saif", output_path, state_name, saif_state_counter);
931:     $display("Starting SAIF recording for state %s at time %t", state_name, $time);
932:     $display("SAIF file: %s", saif_filename);
933:     
934:     // Start SAIF recording
935:     $set_toggle_region(tb_qracc_top.u_qr_acc_top);
936:     $toggle_start();
937:     // $toggle_reset();
938: endtask
939: 
940: // Task to stop SAIF recording and export file
941: task stop_saif_recording(input string state_name);
942:     $display("Stopping SAIF recording for state %s at time %t", state_name, $time);
943:     
944:     // Stop SAIF recording and dump to file
945:     $toggle_stop();
946:     $toggle_report(saif_filename, 1.0e-9, tb_qracc_top.u_qr_acc_top);
947:     $toggle_reset();
948:     
949:     saif_state_counter++;
950: endtask
951: 
952: task restart_saif_recording(input string state_name);
953:     saif_filename = $sformatf("%s/saif_%s_%0d.saif", output_path, state_name, saif_state_counter);
954:     $display("Restarting SAIF recording for state %s at time %t", state_name, $time);    
955:     $toggle_start();
956: endtask
957: 
958: // Monitor QRAcc controller state changes
959: always @(posedge clk) begin
960:     if (!nrst) begin
961:         saif_initialized <= 0;
962:         saif_state_counter <= 0;
963:         previous_state <= 0;
964:         current_state <= 0;
965:     end else begin
966:         current_state <= u_qr_acc_top.u_qracc_controller.csr_main_internal_state;
967:         
968:         if (!saif_initialized) begin
969:             previous_state <= current_state;
970:             saif_initialized <= 1;
971:             start_saif_recording($sformatf("state_%0d", current_state));
972:         end else if (current_state != previous_state) begin
973:             // State changed - stop current recording and start new one
974:             stop_saif_recording($sformatf("state_%0d", previous_state));
975:             restart_saif_recording($sformatf("state_%0d", current_state));
976:             previous_state <= current_state;
977:         end
978:     end
979: end
980: 
981: // Final SAIF export at end of simulation
982: final begin
983:     if (saif_initialized) begin
984:         stop_saif_recording($sformatf("state_%0d", current_state));
985:         $display("Final SAIF recording completed. Total state recordings: %0d", saif_state_counter);
986:     end
987: end
988: 
989: `endif
990: `endif
991: 
992: // ========================= END SAIF TRACKING SECTION ==========================
993:

1021: end

219: ) u_seq_acc (

222:

228:     .valid_o        (qracc_output_valid),
229:     .mac_data_o     (qracc_mac_output),

233:     .from_analog_i  (from_analog),

237:     .to_sram_rq_valid           (to_sram_rq_valid),

239:     .to_sram_wr_data            (to_sram_wr_data_ctl),
240:     .to_sram_addr               (to_sram_addr),
241:     .from_sram_rq_ready         (from_sram_rq_ready),
242:     .from_sram_rd_valid         (from_sram_rd_valid),
243:     .from_sram_rd_data          (from_sram_rd_data)
244: );

246: // Row staging: assemble numPlanes*numCols-bit rows from hostBusBits-wide LOADs
247: always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge nrst) begin : rowStageRegs
248:     if (!nrst) begin
249:         row_stage_q <= '0;
250:     end else begin
251:         if (bus_req_i.valid && bus_req_i.wen
252:             && (bus_req_i.addr & 32'hFFFFFF00) == 32'h00000100
253:             && cfg.wr_bus_sel != 0) begin
254:             int word_idx;
255:             word_idx = bus_req_i.addr[2:0];
256:             row_stage_q[word_idx*hostBusBits +: hostBusBits] <= bus_req_i.data_in;
257:         end
258:     end
259: end
260: 
261: // Row complete: last word of staged row
262: wire row_complete_pulse = bus_req_i.valid && bus_req_i.wen
263:     && (bus_req_i.addr & 32'hFFFFFF00) == 32'h00000100
264:     && cfg.wr_bus_sel != 0
265:     && bus_req_i.addr[2:0] == (numPlanes*numCols/hostBusBits - 1);
266: 
267: // Complete row value including the current LOAD word (valid when row_complete_pulse)
268: logic [numPlanes*numCols-1:0] complete_row;
269: always_comb begin : completeRowComb
270:     complete_row = row_stage_q;
271:     if (row_complete_pulse)
272:         complete_row[bus_req_i.addr[2:0]*hostBusBits +: hostBusBits] = bus_req_i.data_in;
273: end
274: 
275: // SRAM write mux: staging logic overrides controller during multibit row writes
276: always_comb begin : sramWriteMux
277:     to_sram_rq_wr = wr_ctrl_rq_wr;
278:     to_sram_rq_valid = wr_ctrl_rq_valid;
279:     to_sram_addr = wr_ctrl_addr;
280:     to_sram_wr_data_ctl = '0;
281:     to_sram_wr_data_ctl[31:0] = legacy_wr_data;
282: 
283:     if (cfg.wr_bus_sel != 0 && row_complete_pulse) begin
284:         int row;
285:         row = bus_req_i.addr[10:3];
286:         to_sram_rq_wr = 1'b1;
287:         to_sram_rq_valid = 1'b1;
288:         to_sram_addr = row;
289:         to_sram_wr_data_ctl = '0;
290:         to_sram_wr_data_ctl[row*256 +: 256] = complete_row;
291:     end
292: end
293: 
294: always_comb begin : wsAccDataOrientation
295:     // Orient the data for the WSAcc PE cluster
296:     for (int i = 0; i < wsAccNumPes; i++) begin // Channels
297:         for (int j = 0; j < wsAccWindowElements; j++) begin // Window
298:             wsacc_data_i[i][j] = feature_loader_data_out[i + j * cfg.num_input_channels];
299:         end
300:     end
301: end
302: 
303: logic wsacc_o_ready;
304: assign wsacc_o_ready = 1;
305: 
306: wsacc_pe_cluster #(
307:     .numPes(wsAccNumPes),
308:     .windowElements(wsAccWindowElements),
309:     .outputWidth(qrAccAccumulatorBits)
310: ) u_wsacc (
311:     .clk(clk),
312:     .nrst(nrst),
313:     .data_i(wsacc_data_i),
314:     .data_i_valid(qracc_ctrl.wsacc_data_i_valid),
315:     .data_i_ready(), // wsacc_pe_cluster will never stall in this system i just added it for fun
316:     
317:     .weight_itf_i(bus_req_i.data_in),
318:     .weight_itf_i_valid(qracc_ctrl.wsacc_weight_itf_i_valid),
319:     .weight_writing_done(),
320: 
321:     .data_o(wsacc_data_o),

482:         piso_write_queue_addr_in[i] = qracc_ctrl.activation_buffer_int_wr_addr + (i * numCols);

490:     .maxBits             (8),

492: ) u_piso_write_queue (
493:     .clk            (clk),
494:     .nrst           (nrst),
495:

497:     .addr_in        (piso_write_queue_addr_in),
498:     .valid_in       (piso_write_queue_valid_in),
499:     .ready_out      (activation_buffer_wr_ready),
500: 
501:     .addr_out       (activation_buffer_int_wr_addr), // Writing to internal interface
502:     .data_out       (activation_buffer_int_wr_data), // Writing to internal interface
503:     .valid_out      (int_write_queue_valid_out) // Valid signal for internal interface
504: );

512:         bus_resp_o = controller_bus_resp;

2: // Wraps the analog QR accelerator
3: // Performs a 1-bit bipolar MAC operation with parametrizable output precision
4: 
5: import qracc_pkg::*;
6: 
7: module qr_acc_wrapper #(
8:     parameter numRows = 128,
9:     parameter outputElements = 128,
10:     parameter numCols = 32,
11:     parameter numAdcBits = 4,
12:     parameter int numBeatRows = 4,
13:     parameter compCount = (2**numAdcBits)-1 // An ADC only has 2^numAdcBits-1 comparators
14: ) (

16:     input nrst,
17: 
18:     // CONFIG
19:     input qracc_config_t cfg,
20: 
21:     input from_analog_t from_analog_i,

23: 
24:     // DIGITAL INTERFACE: MAC

26:     input mac_en_i,
27:     input [numRows-1:0] data_p_i,
28:     input [numRows-1:0] data_n_i,
29:     
30:     // SRAM signals
31:     input to_sram_rq_wr,
32:     input to_sram_rq_valid,

36:     output logic from_sram_rq_ready,
37:     output logic from_sram_rd_valid,

39: );

67:         // In binary config, the NSM always points to VRST

129:     .wr_data_q              (to_analog_o.WR_DATA),

135:     .c3sram_nprecharge_o   (to_analog_o.PCH),
136:     .c3sram_wl_o           (to_analog_o.WL)
137: );

140:

142:     // If mac is enabled, the ADCs and the array performs MAC no matter what
143:     if (mac_en_i) stats.statSeqAccMacs++;
144:     if (wc_write) stats.statSeqAccWeightWrites++;
145:     // currently we don't track reads, I mean who needs to for an IMC accelerator?
146: end
147: 
148: `endif

151:     if (!nrst) begin

153:         from_sram_rd_valid <= 0;
154:     end else begin

157:     end
158: end

160: // ADC INTERFACE

2: // Write Controller Interface
3: // This module controls writes and reads to a C3SRAM array
4: // 
5: // To write:
6: // 1. Wait for ready to be high
7: // 2. Assert write_i and set addr_i to target row
8: // 3. Wait for done to be asserted
9: //
10: // To read:
11: // 1. Wait for ready to be high
12: // 2. Assert read_i and set addr_i to target row
13: // 3. Wait for done to be asserted
14: //
15: // Note: write_i and read_i should not be asserted simultaneously
16: // wr_bus_sel_i selects the beat width in rows: 0 -> 1 row, 1 -> 2 rows, 2 -> 4 rows.
17: // The whole beat is registered in beat_q and wr_data_q drives the current row slice.
18: `timescale 1ns/1ps
19: 
20: import qracc_pkg::*;
21: 
22: module wr_controller #(
23:     parameter int numRows = 128,
24:     parameter int numCols = 32,
25:     parameter int numPlanes = 8,
26:     parameter int numBeatRows = 4
27: ) (
28:     input clk,
29:     input nrst,
30: 
31:     // we don't passively read in the interest of energy
32:     input write_i,
33:     input read_i,
34:     input [1:0] wr_bus_sel_i,
35:     input [$clog2(numRows)-1:0] addr_i,
36:     input [numBeatRows*numCols-1:0] wr_data_i,
37: 
38:     output logic done, //asserted for just 1 CC
39:     output logic ready,
40:     output logic [numCols-1:0] wr_data_q,
41: 
42:     // writes and reads
43:     output logic [numCols-1:0] c3sram_csel_o,
44:     output logic c3sram_saen_o,
45:     output logic c3sram_w2b_o,
46:     output logic c3sram_nprecharge_o,
47:     output logic [numRows-1:0] c3sram_wl_o
48: );
49: 
50: typedef enum logic [2:0] { 
51:     S_IDLE = 0, 
52:     S_WRITING = 1, 
53:     S_READING = 2 
54: } wr_ctrl_state_t;
55: 
56: wr_ctrl_state_t state_q;
57: wr_ctrl_state_t state_d;
58: 
59: logic [1:0] pos_ctr;
60: logic [$clog2(numRows)-1:0] addr_i_q;
61: logic [1:0] rows_per_beat;
62: logic [1:0] row_idx_q;
63: logic [numBeatRows*numCols-1:0] beat_q;
64: 
65: // FSM
66: always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge nrst) begin
67:     if (!nrst) begin
68:         state_q <= S_IDLE;
69:     end else begin
70:         state_q <= state_d;
71:     end
72: end
73: always_comb begin
74:     case (state_q)
75:         S_IDLE: begin
76:             case({write_i,read_i})
77:                 default: state_d = S_IDLE;
78:                 2'b10:   state_d = S_WRITING;
79:                 2'b01:   state_d = S_READING;
80:             endcase
81:         end
82:         S_WRITING, S_READING: begin 
83:             if (pos_ctr == 0 && row_idx_q == rows_per_beat-1'b1)
84:                 state_d = S_IDLE;
85:             else
86:                 state_d = state_q;
87:         end 
88:         default: state_d = state_q;
89:     endcase
90: end
91: 
92: // Data registering when writing
93: always_ff @( posedge clk ) begin : wrDataReg
94:     if (!nrst) begin
95:         beat_q <= 0;
96:         addr_i_q <= 0;
97:     end else begin
98:         if (state_q == S_IDLE) begin
99:             if (state_d == S_WRITING) begin
100:                 beat_q <= wr_data_i;
101:                 addr_i_q <= addr_i;
102:             end
103:             if (state_d == S_READING) begin
104:                 addr_i_q <= addr_i;
105:             end
106:         end 
107:     end
108: end
109: 
110: // Row index within the current beat
111: always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge nrst) begin : rowIndex
112:     if (!nrst) begin
113:         row_idx_q <= 0;
114:     end else begin
115:         if (state_q == S_IDLE) begin
116:             row_idx_q <= 0;
117:         end else if (state_q == S_WRITING || state_q == S_READING) begin
118:             if (pos_ctr == 0 && row_idx_q != rows_per_beat-1'b1)
119:                 row_idx_q <= row_idx_q + 1;
120:         end
121:     end
122: end
123: 
124: // Position Counter
125: always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge nrst) begin : positionCounter
126:     if (!nrst) begin
127:         pos_ctr <= 3;
128:     end else begin
129:         case (state_q)
130:             S_IDLE: pos_ctr <= 3;
131:             S_WRITING, S_READING: pos_ctr <= pos_ctr - 1;
132:             default: pos_ctr <= pos_ctr;
133:         endcase
134:     end
135: end
136: 
137: // Rows per beat based on bus select
138: always_comb begin
139:     case (wr_bus_sel_i)
140:         2'd1: rows_per_beat = 2; // 64B beat
141:         2'd2: rows_per_beat = 4; // 128B beat
142:         default: rows_per_beat = 1; // 32B beat
143:     endcase
144: end
145: 
146: // Output row of the current beat (combinational slice, no X on reset)
147: always_comb begin
148:     wr_data_q = beat_q[row_idx_q*numCols +: numCols];
149: end
150: 
151: localparam w2bWaveWr  = 4'b1111;
152: localparam nprechargeWaveWr = 4'b1111;
153: localparam targetWlWaveWr = 4'b0110;
154: 
155: localparam saenWaveRd = 4'b0010;
156: // localparam cselWaveRd = 4'b1010;
157: localparam cselWaveRd = 4'b0000;
158: localparam nprechargeWaveRd = 4'b1110;
159: localparam targetWlWaveRd = 4'b0110;
160: 
161: // C3SRAM Signals
162: always_comb  begin
163:     case (state_q)
164:         default: begin
165:             c3sram_csel_o = 0;
166:             c3sram_saen_o = 0;
167:             c3sram_w2b_o = 0;
168:             c3sram_wl_o = 0;
169:             c3sram_nprecharge_o = 0;
170:         end
171:         S_READING: begin
172:             c3sram_csel_o = {numCols*{cselWaveRd[pos_ctr]}};
173:             c3sram_saen_o = saenWaveRd[pos_ctr];
174:             c3sram_w2b_o = 0;
175:             c3sram_wl_o = 0;
176:             c3sram_wl_o[addr_i_q + row_idx_q] = targetWlWaveRd[pos_ctr];
177:             c3sram_nprecharge_o = nprechargeWaveRd[pos_ctr];
178:         end
179:         S_WRITING: begin
180:             c3sram_csel_o = 0;
181:             c3sram_saen_o = 0;
182:             c3sram_w2b_o = w2bWaveWr[pos_ctr];
183:             c3sram_wl_o = 0;
184:             c3sram_wl_o[addr_i_q + row_idx_q] = targetWlWaveWr[pos_ctr];
185:             c3sram_nprecharge_o = nprechargeWaveWr[pos_ctr];
186:         end
187:     endcase
188: end
189: 
190: // Done: asserted only on the last row
191: always_comb ready = (state_q == S_IDLE) ? 1 : 0;
192: always_comb begin 
193:     logic last_row;
194:     last_row = (row_idx_q == rows_per_beat-1'b1);
195:     case (state_q)
196:         S_READING:
197:             done = (pos_ctr == 1) && last_row;
198:         default: begin
199:             done = (pos_ctr == 0) && last_row;
200:         end
201:     endcase
202: end
203: 
204: endmodule

3: n-Bit input MAC accelerator

7: `timescale 1ns/1ps
8: 
9: import qracc_pkg::*;
10: 
11: module seq_acc #(
12:     parameter maxInputBits = qrAccMaxInputBits,
13:     parameter inputElements = 128,
14:     parameter outputElements = 32,
15:     
16:     parameter numCols = 32,
17:     parameter int numPlanes = 8,

28:     // Data
29:     input [inputElements-1:0][maxInputBits-1:0] mac_data_i,
30:     input mac_valid_i,
31:     output logic ready_o,

33:     // Valid but no ready because we cannot stall

36: 
37:     // Passthrough signals
38:     output to_analog_t to_analog_o [numPlanes-1:0],
39:     input from_analog_t from_analog_i [numPlanes-1:0],
40:     
41:     // SRAM signals
42:     input to_sram_rq_wr,

44:     input [1:0] wr_bus_sel_i,
45:     input [numBeatRows*numPlanes*numCols-1:0] to_sram_wr_data,

50: );

52: // Parameters

54: 
55: // Signals
56: logic [pipelineStages-1:0] pipeline_tracker;
57: logic signed [inputElements-1:0][maxInputBits-1:0] piso_buffer_n_q;
58: logic signed [inputElements-1:0][maxInputBits-1:0] piso_buffer_p_q;

168:             `endif
169:             piso_buffer_n_q <= piso_buffer_n_d;
170:             piso_buffer_p_q <= piso_buffer_p_d;
171:         end else begin
172:             for (int i = 0; i < inputElements; i++) begin
173:                 piso_buffer_n_q[i] <= piso_buffer_n_q[i] >> 1;
174:                 piso_buffer_p_q[i] <= piso_buffer_p_q[i] >> 1;
175:             end
176:         end
177:     end
178: 
179:     // Pipeline tracker
180:     if (!nrst) begin
181:         pipeline_tracker <= '0;
182:     end else begin
183:         if (mac_valid_i && ready_o) begin
184:             pipeline_tracker[0] <= '1;
185:         end else begin
186:             pipeline_tracker[0] <= '0;
187:         end
188:         // Left shift
189:         pipeline_tracker[pipelineStages-1:1] <= pipeline_tracker[pipelineStages-2:0];
190:     end
191: 
192:     // Accumulator
193:     if (!nrst) begin
194:         accumulator <= '0;
195:     end else begin
196:         for (int i = 0; i < outputElements; i++) begin
197:             if (pipeline_tracker[1]) begin // == 'h02
198:                 accumulator[i] <= accumulatorBits'(signed'(tree_sum[i])) << (input_trits - 1); // bit 0 result
199:             end else begin
200:                 accumulator[i] <= {accumulator[i][accumulatorBits-1],accumulator[i][accumulatorBits-1:1]} + ( accumulatorBits'(signed'(tree_sum[i])) << (input_trits - 1) );
201:             end
202:         end
203:     end
204: end
205: 
206: // Datapaths
207: logic [pipelineStages-1:0] ready_mask;
208: logic [pipelineStages-1:0] valid_mask;
209: logic [pipelineStages-1:0] mac_en_mask;
210: 
211: always_comb begin : seqAccDpath
212: 
213:     // Ready mask looks for workload in the first input_trits-1 stages
214:     ready_mask = 2**(input_trits-1) - 1;
215:     // MAC enable looks for workload in the first input_trits stages
216:     mac_en_mask = 2**input_trits - 1;
217:     // Valid mask looks for workload in the last stage
218:     valid_mask = 2**(input_trits+1);

223:     // If something is in the first input_trits stages of the pipeline, we need to enable the MAC

228:     end

233:         data_n_i[i] = piso_buffer_n_q[i][0];
234:     end
235: end

1: // Combine per-plane partial sums by shifting each plane b left by b bits.
2: // Planes b >= weight_bits_i contribute zero (weight_bits_i == 0 treated as B=1).
3: // If weight_signed_i == 1, the MSB plane (b == B-1) is negated before adding.
4: // For B=1 and unsigned, sum_o equals partial_i[0] sign-extended exactly.
5: module shift_add_tree #(
6:     parameter int numPlanes = 8,
7:     parameter int numOutputs = 32,
8:     parameter int partialBits = 4
9: ) (
10:     input  logic [3:0]                      weight_bits_i,
11:     input  logic                            weight_signed_i,
12:     input  logic signed [numPlanes-1:0][numOutputs-1:0][partialBits-1:0] partial_i,
13:     output logic signed [numOutputs-1:0][partialBits+numPlanes-1:0]     sum_o
14: );
15:     localparam int sumBits = partialBits + numPlanes - 1;
16: 
17:     logic [3:0] B;
18:     assign B = (weight_bits_i == 4'd0) ? 4'd1 : weight_bits_i;
19: 
20:     logic signed [sumBits-1:0] terms [numPlanes-1:0][numOutputs-1:0];
21: 
22:     genvar b, o;
23:     generate
24:         for (b = 0; b < numPlanes; b++) begin : g_plane
25:             for (o = 0; o < numOutputs; o++) begin : g_out
26:                 always_comb begin
27:                     if (b >= B) begin
28:                         terms[b][o] = '0;
29:                     end else begin
30:                         terms[b][o] = $signed({{(sumBits-partialBits){partial_i[b][o][partialBits-1]}}, partial_i[b][o]}) << b;
31:                         if (weight_signed_i && (b == B - 1)) begin
32:                             terms[b][o] = -terms[b][o];
33:                         end
34:                     end
35:                 end
36:             end
37:         end
38:     endgenerate
39: 
40:     generate
41:         for (o = 0; o < numOutputs; o++) begin : g_sum
42:             always_comb begin
43:                 sum_o[o] = '0;
44:                 for (int b = 0; b < numPlanes; b++) begin
45:                     sum_o[o] += terms[b][o];
46:                 end
47:             end
48:         end
49:     endgenerate
50: 
51: endmodule

7: `timescale 1ns / 1ps
8: 
9: module output_scaler_set #(
10:     parameter numElements = 64,
11:     parameter inputWidth = 20,
12:     parameter outputWidth = 8, 
13:     // Changing these will change the algorithm behavior
14:     parameter scaleBits = 16, 
15:     parameter shiftBits = 4,
16:     parameter offsetBits = 8
17: ) (
18:     input clk, nrst,

21:     output logic signed [numElements-1:0][outputWidth-1:0] y_o,
22: 
23:     // Scaler memory inputs
24:     input scale_w_en_i,
25:     input [scaleBits-1:0] scale_w_data_i,
26: 
27:     // Shift memory inputs
28:     input shift_w_en_i,
29:     input [shiftBits-1:0] shift_w_data_i,

57: // Modules
58: always_ff @( posedge clk or negedge nrst) begin : scalerMemory
59:     if (!nrst) begin
60:         for (int i = 0; i < numElements; i = i + 1) begin
61:             // For verilator, nonblocking assignments cause issues inside for loops (%Error-BLKLOOPINIT)
62:             output_scale[i] = 0;
63:             output_shift[i] = 0;
64:             output_offset[i] = 0;
65:             output_bias[i] = 0;
66:         end
67:         scale_w_addr <= 0;

69:         offset_w_addr <= 0;
70:         bias_w_addr <= 0;

74:             scale_w_addr <= scale_w_addr + 1;
75:         end
76:         if (shift_w_en_i) begin
77:             output_shift[shift_w_addr] <= shift_w_data_i;
78:             shift_w_addr <= scale_w_addr + 1;
79:         end

82:             offset_w_addr <= offset_w_addr + 1;
83:         end
84:         if (bias_w_en_i) begin
85:             output_bias[bias_w_addr] <= bias_w_data_i;
86:             bias_w_addr <= bias_w_addr + 1;
87:         end
88:     end
89: end

93:     for (i = 0; i < numElements; i = i + 1) begin : oScalerInstances

12:     parameter inputWidth = 20,

14:     // Static Parameters, mostly

16:     parameter shiftBits = 16
17: ) (

19: 
20:     input signed [inputWidth-1:0] wx_i,

27: 
28:     input cfg_unsigned,
29:     input [3:0] cfg_output_bits
30: );
31: 
32: logic signed [maxOutputWidth-1:0] saturateHigh;
33: logic signed [maxOutputWidth-1:0] saturateLow;
34: logic signed [inputWidth-1:0] compareHigh;
35: logic signed [inputWidth-1:0] compareLow;
36: 
37: logic signed [31:0] wx_i_extended;
38: logic signed [31:0] wx_i_biased;
39: logic signed [47:0] scaled_wx;
40: logic signed [31:0] scaled_wx_fpshift;
41: logic signed [31:0] scaled_wx_shifted;
42: logic signed [31:0] scaled_wx_presat;
43: logic signed [maxOutputWidth-1:0] y_o_d;
44: 
45: assign y_o = y_o_d;
46: 
47: always_comb begin : saturationLimits
48: 
49:     // Saturation limits
50:     if (cfg_unsigned) begin
51:         saturateHigh = 2**cfg_output_bits - 1;  // 0111...111
52:         saturateLow = 0;  // 0000...000
53:         compareHigh = { {inputWidth-maxOutputWidth{1'b0}}, saturateHigh };
54:         compareLow = { {inputWidth-maxOutputWidth{1'b0}}, saturateLow };
55:     end else begin
56:         saturateHigh = 2**(cfg_output_bits-1) - 1;  // 0111...111
57:         saturateLow = 2**(cfg_output_bits-1);  // 1000...000
58:         compareHigh = { {inputWidth-maxOutputWidth{1'b0}}, saturateHigh };
59:         compareLow = { {inputWidth-maxOutputWidth{1'b1}}, saturateLow };
60:     end
61:     
62: end
63: 
64: always_comb begin : fpMultComb
65:     // We have to explicitly implement this as unsigned multiplications
66:     // Because signed * unsigned asymmetric multiplication is ambiguous
67:     // Note: signed * unsigned --> unsigned * unsigned
68:     // All of this is scary when switching simulators.
69: 
70:     wx_i_extended = { {32-inputWidth{wx_i[inputWidth-1]}}, wx_i };
71:     wx_i_biased = wx_i_extended + output_bias;
72:     
73:     if (wx_i_biased[31] == 1) begin
74:         // wx_i_biased is negative
75:         scaled_wx = ~(48'( inputWidth'(~wx_i_biased+1) * output_scale)) + 1;
76:     end else begin
77:         // wx_i_biased is positive
78:         scaled_wx = 48'(wx_i_biased * output_scale);
79:     end
80: 
81:     // It doesn't do arithmetic shift unless $signed for some reason
82:     // But it doesn't matter for fpshift because it's just a bit drop
83:     scaled_wx_fpshift = scaled_wx >>> fixedPointBits;
84:     
85:     // Arithmetic shift divide by 2**n rounds down towards -infty
86:     // This turns negatives wrong vs python, so we have to explicitly state things
87:     // if (scaled_wx_fpshift[inputWidth-1] == 1) begin
88:     //     scaled_wx_shifted = ($signed(scaled_wx_fpshift) >>> output_shift) + 1;
89:     // end else begin
90:     scaled_wx_shifted = $signed(scaled_wx_fpshift) >>> output_shift;
91:     // end
92: 
93:     scaled_wx_presat = scaled_wx_shifted + {24'b0,output_offset};
94: 
95:     // Saturating clipping
96:     if ($signed(scaled_wx_presat) > compareHigh) begin // Does this auto sign-extend for the comparison?
97:         y_o_d = saturateHigh;
98:     end
99:     else if ($signed(scaled_wx_presat) < compareLow) begin // Does this auto sign-extend?
100:         y_o_d = saturateLow;
101:     end
102:     else begin
103:         y_o_d = scaled_wx_presat[maxOutputWidth-1:0];
104:     end
105: end
106: 
107: endmodule

1: // Twos complement to bipolar converter
2: // 
3: // Output bits require inBits if unsigned, or inBits-1 if signed
4: // 
5: 
6: module twos_to_bipolar #(
7:     parameter inBits = 8,
8:     parameter numLanes = 1
9: ) (
10:     input signed [numLanes-1:0][inBits-1:0] twos,
11:     input [3:0] input_bits,
12:     output logic [numLanes-1:0][inBits-1:0] bipolar_p,
13:     output logic [numLanes-1:0][inBits-1:0] bipolar_n
14: );
15: 
16: logic signed [numLanes-1:0][inBits-1:0] ntwos;
17: always_comb begin : dataPath
18:     for (int i = 0; i < numLanes; i++) begin    
19:         ntwos[i] = -twos[i];
20:         if (twos[i] == 0) begin
21:             bipolar_p[i] = 0;
22:             bipolar_n[i] = 0;
23:         end
24:         else begin
25:             int bit_idx;
26:             bit_idx = (input_bits-1 < inBits) ? input_bits-1 : inBits-1;
27:             if (twos[i][bit_idx]) begin
28:                 bipolar_p[i] = '0;
29:                 bipolar_n[i] = ntwos[i];
30:             end else begin
31:                 bipolar_p[i] = twos[i];
32:                 bipolar_n[i] = '0;
33:             end
34:         end
35:     end
36: end
37: 
38: endmodule

7: module ts_qracc #(
8: 
9:     // These are defined as localparams for ease of change
10:     // But so that we also don't need to set values when synthesizing
11:     parameter numRows = 128,
12:     parameter numCols = 8,
13:     parameter numAdcBits = 4,
14:     parameter int numPlanes = 8,

16: ) (

30:     input logic [numRows-1:0] NSM_VSS_SEL,
31:     input logic [numRows-1:0] NSM_VSS_SELB,
32:     input logic [numRows-1:0] NSM_VRST_SEL,
33:     input logic [numRows-1:0] NSM_VRST_SELB,
34:

40:     input logic WRITE,

48:     input logic M2A,
49:     input logic M2AB,
50:     input logic R2A,
51:     input logic R2AB,
52: 
53:     // Clock
54:     input logic CLK
55: );
56: 
57: // SRAM modelling
58: logic [numCols-1:0] mem [numRows];
59: always_ff @( posedge CLK ) begin :  sramModel
60:     if (PCH) begin
61:         if (WRITE) begin
62:             for (int i = 0; i < numRows; i++) begin
63:                 if (WL[i]) begin 
64:                     mem[i] = WR_DATA; // blocking assignment in behavioral SRAM model
65:                     // $display("WR_DATA: %d, %d", WR_DATA, mem[i]);
66:                 end
67:             end
68:         end
69:     end
70: end
71: // SA output is not registered
72: always_comb begin : readModel
73:     if (PCH && SAEN) begin
74:         for (int i = 0; i < numRows; i++) begin
75:             if (WL[i]) SA_OUT = mem[i];
76:         end
77:     end else begin
78:         SA_OUT = 0;
79:     end
80: end
81: 
82: 
83: // MAC modelling
84: logic signed [31:0] mbl_value [numCols];
85: logic signed [numAdcBits-1:0] adc_out [numCols];
86: logic signed [numCols-1:0][compCount-1:0] comp_out;
87: logic doiroundup[numCols];
88: 
89: 
90: localparam signed maxValueAdc = {1'b0, {(numAdcBits-1){1'b1}}};  // 0111
91: localparam signed minValueAdc = {1'b1, {(numAdcBits-1){1'b0}}};  // 1000
92: // localparam signed maxValue = {1'b0, {(numAdcBits+adc_ref_range_shifts-1){1'b1}}};  // 0111
93: // localparam signed minValue = {1'b1, {(numAdcBits+adc_ref_range_shifts-1){1'b0}}};  // 1000
94: 
95: logic signed [31:0] maxValue;
96: logic signed [31:0] minValue;
97: always_comb begin : maxMinValues
98:     // The max and min values are defined by the ADC bits and the reference range shifts
99:     maxValue = 2**(numAdcBits+adc_ref_range_shifts-1)-1;
100:     minValue = 2**(numAdcBits+adc_ref_range_shifts-1) * -1;
101: end
102: 
103: always_comb begin : toMBL
104:     for (int j = 0; j < numCols; j++) begin
105:         mbl_value[j] = 0;
106:         for (int i = 0; i < numRows; i++) begin
107:             if(mem[i][j] == 1) begin
108:                 mbl_value[j] += PSM_VDR_SEL[i];
109:                 mbl_value[j] -= PSM_VSS_SEL[i];
110:             end else begin 
111:                 mbl_value[j] += NSM_VDR_SEL[i];
112:                 mbl_value[j] -= NSM_VSS_SEL[i];
113:             end
114:             // $display("mbl_value[%d] = %d", j, mbl_value[j]);
115:         end
116:         
117:         // ADC reference voltage range is divided by 2**NUM_ADC_REF_RANGE_SHIFTS
118:         // to follow the "best resolution"
119:         // The real circuit doesn't shift, it rounds.e
120:         if(adc_ref_range_shifts == 0) begin
121:             doiroundup[j] = 0;
122:         end else begin
123:             doiroundup[j] = (mbl_value[j][adc_ref_range_shifts-1]);
124:         end
125: 
126:         if (mbl_value[j] > maxValue) begin
127:             // $display("MBL value %d is greater than max value %d", mbl_value[j], maxValue);
128:             adc_out[j] = maxValueAdc;
129:         end else begin
130:             if (mbl_value[j] < minValue) begin
131:                 // $display("MBL value %d is less than min value %d", mbl_value[j], minValue);
132:                 adc_out[j] = minValueAdc;
133:             end else begin
134:                 if (adc_ref_range_shifts > 1) begin
135:                     // $display("MBL value %d is between min %d and max %d", mbl_value[j], minValue, maxValue);
136:                     adc_out[j] = mbl_value[j][adc_ref_range_shifts +: numAdcBits] + doiroundup[j];
137:                 end else begin
138:                     // ties to random
139:                     adc_out[j] = mbl_value[j][adc_ref_range_shifts +: numAdcBits];// + $random % (doiroundup[j] + 1);
140:                 end
141:             end
142:         end
143:     end
144:     
145:     // Decode the ADC output
146:     for (int j = 0; j < numCols; j++) begin
147:         for (int i = 0; i < compCount; i++) begin
148:             comp_out[j][i] = ($signed(adc_out[j])+8 > i) ? 1 : 0;
149:         end
150:     end
151:     
152:     ADC_OUT = comp_out;
153: end
154: 
155: endmodule

1: // Element-addressable feature loader
2: // Actually just a regfile
3: `timescale 1ns/1ps
4: 
5: import qracc_pkg::*;
6: 
7: module feature_loader #(
8:     parameter inputWidth    = 256   ,
9:     parameter addrWidth     = 8     , 
10:     parameter elementWidth  = 8     ,
11:     parameter numElements   = 128   
12: ) (
13:     input clk, nrst,
14: 
15:     input logic [inputWidth-1:0]    data_i  ,
16:     input logic [addrWidth-1:0]     addr_i  ,
17:     input logic wr_en,
18: 
19:     output logic [numElements-1:0][elementWidth-1:0] data_o,
20: 
21:     input logic [15:0] mask_start,
22:     input logic [15:0] mask_end
23: );
24: 
25: logic [elementWidth-1:0] staging_register [numElements];
26: 
27: always_ff @( posedge clk or negedge nrst ) begin : writeDecode
28:     if (!nrst) begin
29:         for (int i = 0; i < numElements; i++) begin
30:             // For verilator, nonblocking assignments cause issues inside for loops (%Error-BLKLOOPINIT)
31:             staging_register[i] = 0;
32:         end
33:     end else begin
34:         if (wr_en) begin            
35:             `ifdef TRACK_STATISTICS
36:             stats.statFLWrites++;
37:             `endif
38:             for (int i = 0; i < inputWidth/elementWidth; i++) begin
39:                 // For verilator, nonblocking assignments cause issues inside for loops (%Error-BLKLOOPINIT)
40:                 staging_register[addr_i + i] = data_i[(inputWidth/elementWidth-1-i)*elementWidth +: elementWidth];
41:             end
42:         end
43:     end
44: end
45: 
46: always_comb begin : outDecode
47:     for (int i = 0; i < numElements; i++) begin
48:         if (i >= mask_start && i < mask_end) begin
49:             data_o[i] = staging_register[i];
50:         end else begin
51:             data_o[i] = 0;
52:         end
53:     end
54: end
55: 
56: endmodule

77: def empty_directory(directory):
78:     """
79:     Empty the directory, creating it if necessary.
80:     """
81:     os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)
82:     for filename in os.listdir(directory):
83:             file_path = os.path.join(directory, filename)
84:             try:
85:                 if os.path.isfile(file_path) or os.path.islink(file_path):
86:                     os.unlink(file_path)
87:                 elif os.path.isdir(file_path):
88:                     os.rmdir(file_path)
89:             except Exception as e:
90:                 print(f'Failed to delete {file_path}. Reason: {e}')
91: 
92: def run_simulation(simulator,parameter_list,package_list,tb_file,sim_args,rtl_file_list,log_file,run=True,top_module=None):
93:     """Compile and run an RTL simulation, then assert TEST SUCCESS in the log.
94: 
95:     Parameters
96:     ----------
97:     simulator : str
98:         One of 'vcs', 'xrun', or 'verilator'.
99:     parameter_list : list[str] | dict
100:         Design parameters.  Lists use 'NAME=VALUE' strings; dicts map name->value.
101:     package_list : list[str]
102:         SystemVerilog package files to compile first.
103:     tb_file : str
104:         Path to the testbench source file (relative to ``sims/``).
105:     sim_args : dict
106:         Mapping of simulator name to its argument list.
107:     rtl_file_list : list[str]
108:         RTL source files to compile.
109:     log_file : str
110:         Path to write simulation stdout.
111:     run : bool
112:         If False, only build the command without executing.
113:     top_module : str | None
114:         Explicit top-module name; inferred from *tb_file* when None.
115:     """
116:     os.makedirs('./sims', exist_ok=True)
117: 
118:     if shutil.which(simulator) is None:
119:         pytest.skip(f"{simulator} not found on PATH (install {simulator} or run inside the devcontainer)")
120: 
121:     if simulator == 'verilator':
122:         _run_verilator_simulation(parameter_list, package_list, tb_file, sim_args, rtl_file_list, log_file, run, top_module)
123:         return
124: 
125:     if simulator == 'vcs':
126:         parameter_list = [f'-pvalue+{tb_file}.'+p for p in parameter_list]
127:     else:
128:         parameter_list = []
129: 
130:     top_command = ['-top',top_module] if top_module else []
131: 
132:     command = [
133:             simulator,
134:             *package_list,
135:             tb_file
136:         ] + sim_args[simulator] + rtl_file_list + parameter_list + top_command
137:     
138:     print(f'====={simulator.upper()} COMMAND=====')
139:     print(' '.join(command))
140:     print('=====LOG FILE========')
141:     print(log_file)
142:     print('=====================')
143: 
144:     
145:     if run:
146:         with open(log_file,'w+') as f:
147:             sim = subprocess.Popen(
148:             command, 
149:             shell=False,
150:             cwd='./sims',
151:             stdout=f
152:             )
153:         retval = sim.wait()
154:         if retval:
155:             get_log(log_file)
156:         assert not retval, get_log_tail(log_file,10)
157: 
158:     # Read log file
159:     with open(log_file,'r+') as f:
160:         f.seek(0)
161:         out = [line for line in f.readlines()]
162:         assert 'TEST SUCCESS\n' in out, get_log_tail(log_file,10)

165: def _run_verilator_simulation(parameter_list, package_list, tb_file, sim_args_dict, rtl_file_list, log_file, run=True, top_module=None):
166:     """Run a Verilator simulation using its two-step compile-then-run flow."""
167: 
168:     # Verilator parameters use -G syntax
169:     if isinstance(parameter_list, dict):
170:         verilator_params = [f'-G{name}={value}' for name, value in parameter_list.items()]
171:     elif isinstance(parameter_list, list):
172:         verilator_params = [f'-G{p}' for p in parameter_list] if parameter_list else []
173:     else:
174:         verilator_params = []
175: 
176:     # Determine top module name

240: def write_parameter_definition_file(parameter_list,filepath):
241:     with open(filepath,'w') as f:
242:         f.write(f'`ifndef PARAMETERS_FILE\n')
243:         f.write(f'`define PARAMETERS_FILE\n')
244:         f.write(f'`define PYTEST_GENERATED_PARAMS\n')
245:         for name, value in parameter_list.items():
246:             f.write(f'`define {name} {value}\n')    
247:         f.write(f'`endif // PARAMETERS_FILE\n')
248: 
249: 
250: def get_log_tail(log_file,nlines):
251:     m = f'See {log_file} for details\n' 
252:     with open(log_file,'r') as f:
253:         lines = f.readlines()[-nlines:]
254:         a = ''.join(lines)
255:     return '\n'.join([m,a])
256: 
257: def get_log(log_file):
258:     with open(log_file,'r') as f:
259:         print(f.read())
260: 
261: def rmse_snr(expected, actual):
262:     """Return (RMSE, SNR in dB) between *expected* and *actual* arrays."""
263:     rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((expected - actual)**2))
264:     snr = 10*np.log10(np.var(expected)/np.var(expected - actual))
265:     print(f'RMSE:{rmse}, SNR:{snr}')
266:     return rmse, snr
267: 
268: def save_scatter_fig(expected, actual, title, filename, enabled=False):
269:     """Save an expected-vs-actual scatter plot to ``images/png/<filename>.png``."""
270:     if not enabled:
271:         return
272:     fig, ax = plt.subplots()
273:     plt.figure(figsize=(4,4))
274:     ax = sns.scatterplot(
275:         x=actual.flatten(), 
276:         y=expected.flatten(),
277:         size=1,
278:         legend=False
279:     )
280:     ax.set_title(title)
281:     ax.set_xlabel('Actual')
282:     ax.set_ylabel('Ideal')
283:     lim = [expected.min(),expected.max()]
284:     sns.lineplot(x=lim,y=lim,color='gray',linestyle='--')
285:     plt.tight_layout()
286:     # plt.savefig(f'images/{filename}.svg') # Saving scatter plots to SVG is a little too slow
287:     plt.savefig(f'images/png/{filename}.png')
288:     plt.close()
289:  
290: def plot_diff_channels(diff, tensor_format='NCHW', filename='diff_channels', bitPrecision=8, save=False):
291:     """
292:     Plots all channels of the diff tensor in a subplot grid.
293: 
294:     Parameters:
295:     - diff: numpy.ndarray, the tensor to plot
296:     - tensor_format: str, format of the tensor ('NCHW' or 'NHWC')
297:     - bitPrecision: int, bit precision for value range (default 8)
298:     - save: bool, if False, do not create or save any figure
299:     """
300:     if not save:
301:         return
302:     diff_2plot = np.abs(diff) 
303:     if tensor_format == 'NHWC':
304:         diff_2plot = diff_2plot.transpose(0, 3, 1, 2)  # Convert to NCHW format for plotting
305: 
306:     num_channels = diff_2plot.shape[1]  # Number of channels in the last dimension
307:     rows = (num_channels + 7) // 8  # Calculate rows for 8 columns
308:     fig, axs = plt.subplots(rows, 8, figsize=(20, 10+2.5*(rows-4)), dpi=300)
309:     axs = axs.flatten()  # Flatten the axes array for easier indexing
310:     vmin, vmax = 0, 2**bitPrecision - 1
311: 
312:     for i in range(num_channels):
313:         ax = axs[i]
314:         im = ax.imshow(diff_2plot[0, i], vmin=vmin, vmax=vmax, cmap='CMRmap')
315:         ax.set_title(f'Channel {i}')
316:         ax.axis('off')
317: 
318:     # Hide unused subplots
319:     for j in range(num_channels, len(axs)):
320:         axs[j].axis('off')
321: 
322:     # Unified colorbar with fixed range
323:     plt.subplots_adjust(bottom=0.15)  # Make space for colorbar
324:     cbar = fig.colorbar(im, ax=axs, orientation='horizontal')
325:     cbar.set_label('Difference Value')
326:     plt.suptitle('Difference between Python and HW Results', fontsize=16)
327: 
328:     if save:
329:         plt.savefig(f'images/{filename}.svg', bbox_inches='tight')
330:         plt.savefig(f'images/png/{filename}.png', bbox_inches='tight')
331:     plt.close()

## 1. Python stimulus creation (compile side)

**`qracc_marp/compile/compile.py` — `QrAccNodeCode`**

- `reference_output` is taken **NCHW → NHWC** via transpose `(0, 2, 3, 1)`. This is the golden value Python compares against after RTL readback.
- `_get_weight_data()` (non-depthwise, weight_bits > 1) → `pack_weight_planes(mapped_bin.weights, B=8, signed=True)`.

**`pack_weight_planes` (compile.py)** — this is where the whole B=8 arrangement is decided:

1. `weight_bit_planes(w, 8, signed=True)` → shape `(8, *w.shape)`:
   - `quantize_int8_to_bits` leaves int8 unchanged when `B==8 & signed` (otherwise arithmetic-shift `>> (8-B)` and clamp).
   - `for b in range(8): planes[b] = ((q >> b) & 1)` → plane `b` holds bit `b` of each weight; plane 7 (MSB) is the sign bit; planes `b ≥ B` stay zero.
2. Reshape to `(n_plane, -1)`, pad columns to a multiple of 32, then `reshape(n_plane, -1, 32)`.
3. `array_bin_to_int(rows.reshape(-1,32)[:, ::-1])` — the **`[:, ::-1]` column reversal** makes it MSB-first so matrix column `j` lands at word bit `j`.
4. `words.reshape(n_plane,-1).transpose().reshape(-1)` → flat order **[bank_row, plane]**: word index `i` → bank row `i // 8`, plane `i % 8` (8 words per row).

**`compile()`** emits the command stream:

- 7 CSR LOADs including `CSR_REG_WEIGHT = weight_bits[3:0] | weight_signed << 4`.
- `TRIGGER_LOADWEIGHTS` + `write_weight_rows_to_asm(_get_weight_data())` → each address `base | (row << row_shift) | word` with `row = idx // 8`, `word = idx % 8`; each 32b word holds `row bits [w*32 +:32]` = plane `w` of row `row`.
- Scaler (`pad_scaler_writes`: `scale[mm_offset_x:mm_offset_x+width]`, packed `scaler_data = zp*(2**20) + int_scale*(2**4) + (-shift)`), biases (`pad_bias_data`), ifmap (`pack_ifmap_to_ints` via `as_packed_hex` 4-byte chunks → 32b word).
- `TRIGGER_COMPUTE_ANALOG`, `WAITBUSY`, `TRIGGER_READ_ACTIVATION`, `WAITREAD`, `END`.

**Key python slicing / for loops here:**
- `for b in range(B): planes[b] = ((q >> b) & 1)` — bit-plane extraction.
- `rows.reshape(-1, 32)[:, ::-1]` — column reversal (MSB-first).
- `word i → bank row i//8, plane i%8` — transpose/interleave flat order.
- `pack_ifmap_to_ints`: `for i in range(0, len(a), 4)` — 4 bytes per word.
- `kernel_to_writes`: `word = k3<<24 | k2<<16 | k1<<8 | k0` (element 0 in LSB byte).
- `array_bin_to_int`: `pows = [2**i ...][::-1]` MSB-first bit→int; `binary_array_to_int`: `for bit: out = (out << 1) | bit`.
- Signed reconstruction (mirrors hardware): `r += planes[b]*(1<<b)` for `b < 7`; `r -= planes[7]*(1<<7)` for MSB/sign.

---

## 2. Testbench (`rtl/tb/qracc_top/tb_qracc_top.sv`)

`bus_write_loop()` parses `commands.txt` line by line:
- `LOAD addr data` → drives `bus_req` (wen=1), waits `bus_resp.ready`.
- `WAITBUSY` → polls CSR busy.
- `WAITREAD` → reads `output_fmap_dimx * output_fmap_dimy * num_output_channels` words from `QRACC_MAIN_ADDR` (0x100) into `l2_mem[]`.
- `END` → break.

`export_l2()` writes `l2_mem` to `tb/qracc_top/outputs/hw_ofmap.txt`.

Python readback: `acc_result = np.loadtxt(hw_ofmap.txt).reshape(result_shape)`, then `rmse, snr = rmse_snr(u_code.reference_output, acc_result)`; assert `snr > limit` (1 for `test_rtl`, 8 for `test_marp_multibit` B=8).

---

## 3. RTL hardware path

**Config:** `u_csr` parses bus LOADs into `qracc_config_t cfg`: `weight_bits_cfg[3:0]` (=8), `weight_signed_cfg[4]`, `wr_bus_sel[1:0]`, `adc_ref_range_shifts[7:4]`, dims/offsets/padding.

### Weight write (B > 1 path)
- Address encodes `row = addr[10:3]`, `word = addr[2:0]` (8 words/row).
- Row staging: `row_stage_q[word_idx*32 +: 32] <= data_in`; `row_complete_pulse` when `word_idx == 7` (`256/32 - 1`).
- `complete_row[255:0]` assembled; `to_sram_wr_data_ctl[row*256 +: 256] = complete_row` → **one SRAM row = 256 bits = 8 planes × 32 cols**.
- `plane p` occupies bits `[p*32 +: 32]` within each row.
- `wr_controller` FSM (IDLE → WRITING) drives `WL`, `WRITE`, `c3sram_w2b` wave; `wr_data_q = beat_q[row_idx_q*32 +: 32]`.
- `seq_acc` instantiates **8 `qr_acc_wrapper` (one per plane)**; each slices `planeWr[r*32 +: 32] = to_sram_wr_data[r*256 + p*32 +: 32]` (interleaved plane layout).

### MAC (analog toeplitz array, digital model in `ts_qracc`/`qr_acc_wrapper`/`seq_acc`)
- `twos_to_bipolar` converts activations to bipolar; PISO buffers shift right 1/cycle → **LSB-first serial**: `data_p_i[i] = piso_buffer_p_q[i][0]`.
- `pipeline_tracker` gates `mac_en` / `ready_o` / `valid_o` over `input_trits` stages.
- Each plane's wrapper drives switch-matrix PSM/NSM SEL from `data_p_i`/`data_n_i`; `ts_qracc` SRAM model: on `PCH+WRITE+WL[i]`, `mem[i] = WR_DATA`.
- Per-column MAC: `mbl_value[j] += (mem[i][j]==1 ? PSM-NSM terms)` summed over all rows.
- ADC clamps to `[minValue, maxValue]` set by `adc_bits + adc_ref_range_shifts`, extracts `mbl_value[adc_ref_range_shifts +: numAdcBits]`, 15 comparators per column, `AdcEncoderLogic` → **4-bit signed (-8..7)**.

### Shift-add tree (plane combination)
- `terms[b][o] = sign_extend(partial[b][o]) << b`; if `weight_signed && b == B-1` (i.e. `b == 7`) **negate**; planes `b ≥ B` zeroed.
- `sum_o[o] = sum_b terms[b][o]` → for B=8 signed: `sum_{b=0}^{6} plane_b << b  -  plane_7 << 7`. This exactly mirrors the Python signed reconstruction above.

### Accumulator (sequential over input bit planes)
- `accumulator <= tree_sum << (input_trits-1)` on first cycle; else `(accumulator >> 1 sign-extended) + (tree_sum << (input_trits-1))` — repeated right-shift-add.
- `mac_data_o[i] = accumulator[i] << cfg.adc_ref_range_shifts`.

### Output scaling (`B=8 non-depthwise` → `qracc_mac_output` path)
- `output_scaler_set`: per-element regs `output_scale(16b)`, `output_shift(4b)`, `output_offset(8b)`, `output_bias(32b)` written from `bus_req_i.data_in` slices during scaler/bias LOADs.
- `output_scaler`: `y = clip(((sign_ext(wx) + bias) * scale >>> 16) >>> shift + offset)` → saturate to `cfg_output_bits(8)` signed `[-128, 127]`.
- `mm_output_aligner` + `piso_write_queue` write aligned ofmap to activation buffer (`bank i` addr `wr_addr + i*32`).

---

## 4. Readback to Python
`qracc_controller` issues SRAM reads → TB `WAITREAD` fills `l2_mem` → `export_l2` writes `hw_ofmap.txt` → Python compares vs `reference_output` via `rmse_snr`, asserts SNR.

---

## 5. Key data-arrangement terms (python slicing / for loops)

| Term | Meaning |
|------|---------|
| `[bank_row, plane] flat order` | word index `i` → bank row `i // 8`, plane `i % 8`; unpack loop `plane = i % 8; bank_row = i // 8; f = bank_row*32 + c; planes_out[plane, f] = (int(word) >> c) & 1` |
| Column reversal | `rows.reshape(-1, 32)[:, ::-1]` → MSB-first, matrix col `j` → word bit `j` |
| Signed MSB negation | `r += planes[b]*(1<<b)` for `b < B-1`; `r -= planes[B-1]*(1<<(B-1))` for sign bit (matches `shift_add_tree`) |
| Plane within SRAM row | plane `p` = bits `[p*32 +: 32]` of the 256b row; `to_sram_wr_data[r*256 + p*32 +: 32]` |
| PISO serial | `piso_buffer_q[i] >>= 1` each cycle; `data_p_i[i] = piso_buffer_p_q[i][0]` (LSB first) |
| NCHW ↔ NHWC | transpose `(0, 2, 3, 1)` for `reference_output`/ifmap |
| Scale/bias placement | `scale[mm_offset_x:mm_offset_x + width]`, `biases[mm_offset_x:mm_offset_x + width]` |
| Scaler data packing | `zp*(2**20) + int_scale*(2**4) + (-shift)` |
| Ifmap word packing | `as_packed_hex` 4-byte chunks → 32b word (`for i in range(0, len(a), 4)`) |
| Kernel byte order | `word = k3<<24 \| k2<<16 \| k1<<8 \| k0` (element 0 in LSB byte) |

The central consistency point: the Python bit-plane packing order (`[bank_row, plane]`, column-reversed, signed MSB-negated) and the RTL row staging / plane slicing / shift-add-tree signed negation are mirror images — that's what keeps HW output within SNR of `reference_output`.
